## apps/dna/play.nim — interaction for the 052 Helix port.
##
## Included by app.nim just before `repaint` (an `include`, not an import, so
## this file shares app.nim's scope: `spin`, `seqStr`, `hit`, `txt`, `rrect`…).
## Everything here mirrors 052-dna-helix.html's <script>, second half:
## pointer/wheel/keyboard (html:695-770), sequence handling (html:592-640),
## replication (html:642-693) and the frame loop (html:772-796).
##
## What this adds to the static port:
##   * viz drag-rotate + inertia, buttons/keys zoom, auto-rotate toggle, reset
##   * hover pick with dim + tooltip + seq-strip highlight (both directions)
##   * the below-fold panel: seq input, random, rows, stats, unzip button,
##     legend, strands, footnote — reachable via panel scroll (the DOM scrolls,
##     html:69, so the port scrolls too)
##   * the unzip/replicate state machine with captions
##   * the dark theme toggle
## At rest (no input yet) every one of these is a no-op and the frame is the
## static port's frame: mode=duplex, hover=-1, gScroll=0, light theme.

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── local state
var velPerp = 0.0'f32      ## html:700 fling velocity, rad/s
var pressId = 0            ## pressed control awaiting release (0 = none)
var scrollDrag = false     ## panel scroll in progress
var gSLY = 0.0'f32         ## last scroll-drag y
var focusSeq = false       ## the seq input owns the keyboard
var gBuf = ""              ## seq input buffer (html: `seqIn.value`)
var gMsg = "Type A, T, G or C (2 to 40 bases). The partner strand is generated for you."
var gMsgWarn = false
var gMsgT = 0.0'f32        ## warn timer for the invalid-letter message
var gCap = ""              ## stage caption text ("" = hidden)
var gTip = false           ## tooltip visible
var gTipX, gTipY = 0.0'f32 ## tooltip anchor, viz-local
var gTipI = -1             ## tooltip pair index
var gRng: uint32 = 0x9E3779B9'u32
var gUnzipY = 1140.0'f32   ## unzip button top, recomputed every draw
var gContentBottom = 1460.0'f32  ## panel content end, recomputed every draw

proc rnd(): float32 =
  ## xorshift32 — the freestanding build has no random module.
  gRng = gRng xor (gRng shl 13)
  gRng = gRng xor (gRng shr 17)
  gRng = gRng xor (gRng shl 5)
  float32(gRng shr 8) / 16777216.0'f32

proc fmt1(x: float32): string =
  ## one decimal, no locale formatter (freestanding: no strformat float).
  var v = if x < 0: -x else: x
  var i = int(v)
  var d = int((v - float32(i)) * 10.0'f32 + 0.5'f32)
  if d >= 10: i += 1; d = 0
  (if x < 0: "-" else: "") & $i & "." & $d

proc fmt2(x: float32): string =
  var v = if x < 0: -x else: x
  var i = int(v)
  var d = int((v - float32(i)) * 100.0'f32 + 0.5'f32)
  if d >= 100: i += 1; d = 0
  let ds = if d < 10: "0" & $d else: $d
  (if x < 0: "-" else: "") & $i & "." & ds

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── stats (html:611)
type StatSpan = tuple[pre, bold, post: string]

proc statSpans(): seq[StatSpan] =
  let n = seqStr.len
  var gc = 0
  for c in seqStr:
    if c == 'G' or c == 'C': inc gc
  let at = n - gc
  let tm = if n < 14: float32(2 * at + 4 * gc)
           else: 64.9'f32 + 41.0'f32 * (float32(gc) - 16.4'f32) / float32(n)
  @[("Length ", $n & " bp", " (" & fmt1(float32(n) * 0.34'f32) & " nm)"),
    ("Turns ", fmt2(float32(n) / 10.5'f32), ""),
    ("GC ", $(int(float32(gc) / float32(n) * 100.0'f32 + 0.5'f32)) & "%", ""),
    ("H-bonds ", $(2 * at + 3 * gc), ""),
    ("Tm ≈ ", $int(tm + 0.5'f32) & " °C", " (basic estimate)")]

proc spanW(s: StatSpan; size: float32): float32 =
  textW(s.pre, size) + textW(s.bold, size) * 1.08'f32 + textW(s.post, size)

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── panel layout
## Page coordinates; the caller draws inside translate(0,-gScroll). Derived from
## the stylesheet the way the static port's chrome is, except these elements
## start below the 816px reference frame so there is no ink to measure — the
## CSS box model is the ground truth here.
const
  SEQ_H2_Y   = 890.0'f32   ## "Build a sequence" baseline (facts end 853 + 26 + 11)
  SEQ_LBL_Y  = 919.0'f32   ## label baseline
  SEQ_ROW_Y  = 929.0'f32   ## seqrow top, height 42
  SEQ_MSG_Y  = 989.0'f32   ## .msg baseline (row end 971 + 6 + 12)
  SEQ_BOX_Y  = 1007.0'f32  ## .seq box top (msg end 995 + 12)
  SEQ_ROW_AX = 917.0'f32   ## bases column left (887 + 12 pad + 18 end)
  SEQ_ADV    = 13.26'f32   ## .bases span width: 1.02em at 13px
  SEQ_RA_Y   = 1032.0'f32  ## rowA baseline (box + 12 pad + 13)
  SEQ_RH_Y   = 1050.0'f32  ## rowH baseline
  SEQ_RB_Y   = 1068.0'f32  ## rowB baseline
  SEQ_BOX_H  = 71.0'f32    ## box bottom 1078 (rowB + 10 pad)
  SEQ_IN_PX  = 9.377'f32   ## #seqIn advance: (.5498 + .12) * 14

proc baseCol(c: char): uint32 =
  case c
  of 'A': C_bA
  of 'T': C_bT
  of 'G': C_bGrn
  else: C_bC

proc randBtnW(): float32 =
  28.0'f32 + 16.0'f32 + 8.0'f32 + textW("Random", 13.0'f32)

proc seqInBox(): tuple[x, w: float32] =
  let bw = randBtnW()
  (887.0'f32, PAN_R - 6.0'f32 - bw - 887.0'f32)

proc drawSeqPanel(d: var Draw) =
  ## html:200-225 — everything below MEASUREMENTS. The static port stops at the
  ## facts because the 816px reference frame does; this draws the rest so the
  ## controls the brief requires (sequence input, random, unzip, legend) exist.
  discard tracked(d, PAN_L, SEQ_H2_Y, 11.0'f32, C_INK3, "BUILD A SEQUENCE",
                  0.18'f32 * 11.0'f32, 700)
  txt(d, PAN_L, SEQ_LBL_Y, 13.0'f32, C_INK, "Top strand, read 5′ → 3′", 600)
  ## the input + Random row (html:85-86, 203-206)
  let (ix, iw) = seqInBox()
  let bx = PAN_R - randBtnW()
  let bc = if focusSeq: C_FOCUS else: C_LINE
  rrect(d, ix, SEQ_ROW_Y, iw, 42.0'f32, 10.0'f32, 1.0'f32, bc, C_PANEL2)
  rrect(d, bx, SEQ_ROW_Y, randBtnW(), 42.0'f32, 10.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL)
  ## dice icon (html:205): a 16px rounded square with four pips.
  rrect(d, bx + 14.0'f32, SEQ_ROW_Y + 13.0'f32, 16.0'f32, 16.0'f32, 3.0'f32,
        1.4'f32, C_INK2, C_PANEL)
  for k in 0 .. 3:
    let ox = if (k mod 2) == 0: 4.0'f32 else: 12.0'f32
    let oy = if k < 2: 4.0'f32 else: 12.0'f32
    d = d.circle(bx + 14.0'f32 + ox, SEQ_ROW_Y + 13.0'f32 + oy, 1.1'f32, C_INK2)
  txt(d, bx + 14.0'f32 + 24.0'f32, SEQ_ROW_Y + 27.0'f32, 13.0'f32, C_INK,
      "Random", 600)
  ## input text: 14px mono uppercase, .12em tracking, clipped to the box.
  d = d.save()
  d = d.clip(ix + 12.0'f32, SEQ_ROW_Y, iw - 24.0'f32, 42.0'f32)
  let tw = float32(gBuf.len) * SEQ_IN_PX
  let offX = max(0.0'f32, tw - (iw - 24.0'f32))
  var cx = ix + 12.0'f32 - offX
  for ch in gBuf:
    txt(d, cx, SEQ_ROW_Y + 27.0'f32, 14.0'f32, C_INK, $ch, 400)
    cx += SEQ_IN_PX
  if focusSeq:
    d = d.line(cx + 1.0'f32, SEQ_ROW_Y + 12.0'f32, cx + 1.0'f32,
               SEQ_ROW_Y + 30.0'f32, C_INK, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.restore()
  ## the message line (html:95-96, 207)
  txt(d, PAN_L, SEQ_MSG_Y, 12.0'f32, (if gMsgWarn: C_RED else: C_INK3), gMsg)
  ## the three sequence rows (html:97-106, 208-212)
  rrect(d, PAN_L, SEQ_BOX_Y, PAN_R - PAN_L, SEQ_BOX_H, 12.0'f32, 1.0'f32,
        C_LINE, C_PANEL2)
  let ends = ["5′", "", "3′"]
  let endsR = ["3′", "", "5′"]
  let rowY = [SEQ_RA_Y, SEQ_RH_Y, SEQ_RB_Y]
  for r in 0 .. 2:
    txt(d, 899.0'f32, rowY[r], 11.0'f32, C_INK3, ends[r])
    let er = endsR[r]
    if er.len > 0:
      txt(d, 1242.0'f32 - textW(er, 11.0'f32), rowY[r], 11.0'f32, C_INK3, er)
  d = d.save()
  d = d.clip(SEQ_ROW_AX, SEQ_BOX_Y, 1242.0'f32 - SEQ_ROW_AX, SEQ_BOX_H)
  var i = 0
  while i < seqStr.len:
    let chx = SEQ_ROW_AX + float32(i) * SEQ_ADV
    if hovSeq == i:
      let hl = rgba(27, 111, 216, 0.16'f32)
      rrect(d, chx, SEQ_RA_Y - 14.0'f32, SEQ_ADV, 16.0'f32, 3.0'f32, 0.0'f32,
            hl, hl)
      rrect(d, chx, SEQ_RH_Y - 12.0'f32, SEQ_ADV, 14.0'f32, 3.0'f32, 0.0'f32,
            hl, hl)
      rrect(d, chx, SEQ_RB_Y - 14.0'f32, SEQ_ADV, 16.0'f32, 3.0'f32, 0.0'f32,
            hl, hl)
    let a = seqStr[i]
    let s = $a
    txt(d, chx + (SEQ_ADV - textW(s, 13.0'f32)) * 0.5'f32, SEQ_RA_Y,
        13.0'f32, baseCol(a), s, 700)
    let hb = if a == 'A' or a == 'T': "‖" else: "⦀"
    txt(d, chx + (SEQ_ADV - textW(hb, 10.0'f32)) * 0.5'f32, SEQ_RH_Y,
        10.0'f32, C_INK3, hb)
    let b = compStr[i]
    let bs = $b
    txt(d, chx + (SEQ_ADV - textW(bs, 13.0'f32)) * 0.5'f32, SEQ_RB_Y,
        13.0'f32, baseCol(b), bs, 700)
    inc i
  d = d.restore()
  ## stats (html:107-108, 213): flex-wrap spans, 12px, bold values.
  let spans = statSpans()
  var sy = SEQ_BOX_Y + SEQ_BOX_H + 10.0'f32 + 12.0'f32
  var sx = PAN_L
  for s in spans:
    let w = spanW(s, 12.0'f32)
    if sx + w > PAN_R and sx > PAN_L:
      sx = PAN_L
      sy += 17.0'f32
    txt(d, sx, sy, 12.0'f32, C_INK2, s.pre)
    let x1 = sx + textW(s.pre, 12.0'f32)
    txt(d, x1, sy, 12.0'f32, C_INK, s.bold, 700)
    txt(d, x1 + textW(s.bold, 12.0'f32) * 1.08'f32, sy, 12.0'f32, C_INK2,
        s.post)
    sx += w + 14.0'f32
  ## the unzip button (html:88, 92, 214): full width, 46px, teal→red ramp.
  gUnzipY = sy + 5.0'f32 + 10.0'f32
  let ux = PAN_L
  let uw = PAN_R - PAN_L
  let uh = 46.0'f32
  if mode == 1 or mode == 2 or mode == 4:
    d = d.save()
    d = d.alpha(0.6'f32)
  ## `Draw` has no gradient fill, so the ramp is 2px vertical bands whose ends
  ## are cut back along the 10px corner arcs — exact to the pixel at the edges.
  var bandx = ux
  while bandx < ux + uw:
    let bw = min(2.0'f32, ux + uw - bandx)
    let t = (bandx + bw * 0.5'f32 - ux) / uw
    var r, g, b: float32
    if t < 0.45'f32:
      let k = t / 0.45'f32
      r = 31.0'f32 + (46.0'f32 - 31.0'f32) * k
      g = 179.0'f32 + (196.0'f32 - 179.0'f32) * k
      b = 165.0'f32 + (182.0'f32 - 165.0'f32) * k
    else:
      let k = (t - 0.45'f32) / 0.95'f32
      r = 46.0'f32 + (231.0'f32 - 46.0'f32) * k
      g = 196.0'f32 + (29.0'f32 - 196.0'f32) * k
      b = 182.0'f32 + (54.0'f32 - 182.0'f32) * k
    let dxL = bandx + bw * 0.5'f32 - ux
    let dxR = ux + uw - (bandx + bw * 0.5'f32)
    var cut = 0.0'f32
    if dxL < 10.0'f32:
      cut = max(cut, 10.0'f32 - sqrt(100.0'f32 - dxL * dxL))
    if dxR < 10.0'f32:
      cut = max(cut, 10.0'f32 - sqrt(100.0'f32 - dxR * dxR))
    d = d.rect(bandx, gUnzipY + cut, bw, uh - cut * 2.0'f32,
               rgba(rnd32(r), rnd32(g), rnd32(b), 1.0'f32))
    bandx += bw
  let ulbl = if mode == 0: "Unzip & replicate"
             elif mode == 3: "Rejoin strands"
             elif mode == 4: "Rejoining…"
             else: "Replicating…"
  let ulw = textW(ulbl, 13.0'f32) * 1.08'f32
  let ugx = ux + (uw - (16.0'f32 + 8.0'f32 + ulw)) * 0.5'f32
  d = d.svgPath("M10 2v5M10 7 5 18M10 7l5 11M7.3 12h5.4", ugx,
                gUnzipY + 15.0'f32, 0.8'f32, OV_WHITE, 1.8)
  txt(d, ugx + 24.0'f32, gUnzipY + 29.0'f32, 13.0'f32, OV_WHITE, ulbl, 600)
  if mode == 1 or mode == 2 or mode == 4:
    d = d.restore()
  ## Legend (html:109-112, 217-223).
  let lgH2 = gUnzipY + uh + 26.0'f32 + 11.0'f32
  discard tracked(d, PAN_L, lgH2, 11.0'f32, C_INK3, "LEGEND",
                  0.18'f32 * 11.0'f32, 700)
  const LG = [("Adenine (A)", "purine · pairs with T", C_A),
              ("Thymine (T)", "pyrimidine · pairs with A", C_T),
              ("Guanine (G)", "purine · pairs with C", C_G),
              ("Cytosine (C)", "pyrimidine · pairs with G", C_C)]
  var ly = lgH2 + 12.0'f32 + 13.0'f32
  for r in 0 .. 1:
    for c in 0 .. 1:
      let lx = PAN_L + float32(c) * ((PAN_R - PAN_L - 8.0'f32) * 0.5'f32 + 8.0'f32)
      let e = LG[r * 2 + c]
      d = d.rect(lx, ly - 11.0'f32, 26.0'f32, 12.0'f32, e[2], 4.0'f32)
      txt(d, lx + 36.0'f32, ly, 13.0'f32, C_INK, e[0])
      txt(d, lx + 36.0'f32, ly + 14.0'f32, 11.0'f32, C_INK3, e[1])
    ly += 40.0'f32
  ## strands (html:113-115, 224): three chips, wrapped.
  var stx = PAN_L
  var sty = ly + 9.0'f32
  const ST = ["Top strand", "Partner strand", "Newly built strands"]
  for k in 0 .. 2:
    let w = 18.0'f32 + 6.0'f32 + textW(ST[k], 12.0'f32)
    if stx + w > PAN_R and stx > PAN_L:
      stx = PAN_L
      sty += 20.0'f32
    let sc = if k == 0: C_TEAL elif k == 1: C_RED else: 0xFFBFB7B9'u32
    d = d.rect(stx, sty - 10.0'f32, 18.0'f32, 6.0'f32, sc, 3.0'f32)
    txt(d, stx + 24.0'f32, sty, 12.0'f32, C_INK2, ST[k])
    stx += w + 16.0'f32
  ## footnote (html:116, 225).
  let noteY = sty + 22.0'f32
  let note = "The geometry follows idealised B-DNA: 10.5 bp per turn, 0.34 nm rise, about 2 nm across, with the two backbones offset by roughly 135° around the axis. Replication here is simplified: helicase, primase and polymerase are left out, and both new strands are drawn growing in the same direction."
  let nlines = wrapText(note, 11.5'f32, PAN_R - PAN_L)
  lines(d, PAN_L, noteY, 11.5'f32, 11.5'f32 * 1.55'f32, C_INK3, note,
        PAN_R - PAN_L)
  gContentBottom = noteY + float32(nlines.len) * 11.5'f32 * 1.55'f32 + 30.0'f32

const BASE_NAME = ["Adenine", "Thymine", "Guanine", "Cytosine"]

proc drawCapTip(d: var Draw) =
  ## the stage caption (html:59-61, 168) and the hover tooltip (html:62-66,
  ## 169). Both live in the viz column, in page coordinates.
  if gCap.len > 0:
    let cls = wrapText(gCap, 13.0'f32, 460.0'f32)
    var cw = 0.0'f32
    for s in cls: cw = max(cw, textW(s, 13.0'f32))
    let w = cw + 32.0'f32
    let h = float32(cls.len) * 13.0'f32 * 1.4'f32 + 18.0'f32
    let x = VW - 24.0'f32 - w
    let y = TOP_H + 24.0'f32
    d = d.rect(x, y, w, h, C_INK, 14.0'f32)
    var by = y + 9.0'f32 + 13.0'f32
    for s in cls:
      txt(d, x + 16.0'f32, by, 13.0'f32, C_BG, s, 600)
      by += 13.0'f32 * 1.4'f32
  if gTip and gTipI >= 0 and gTipI < seqStr.len:
    let a = seqStr[gTipI]
    let b = compAt(int32(gTipI))
    let at = a == 'A' or a == 'T'
    let l1b = $a & (if at: "=" else: "≡") & $b
    let l1r = "· pair " & $(gTipI + 1) & " of " & $seqStr.len
    let l2 = BASE_NAME[baseIdx(a)] & " (" &
             (if PUR[baseIdx(a)]: "purine" else: "pyrimidine") & ") with " &
             BASE_NAME[baseIdx(b)] & " (" &
             (if PUR[baseIdx(b)]: "purine" else: "pyrimidine") & ")"
    let l3 = (if at: "2" else: "3") & " hydrogen bonds"
    var l2s = wrapText(l2, 12.0'f32, 226.0'f32)
    if l2s.len == 0: l2s = @[l2]
    var cw = textW(l1b, 15.0'f32) + 4.0'f32 + textW(l1r, 12.0'f32)
    for s in l2s: cw = max(cw, textW(s, 12.0'f32))
    cw = max(cw, textW(l3, 12.0'f32))
    let w = min(250.0'f32, cw + 24.0'f32)
    let h = 9.0'f32 + 20.0'f32 + float32(l2s.len) * 17.4'f32 + 17.4'f32 + 9.0'f32
    var tx = gTipX
    var ty = gTipY
    if tx > VW - 270.0'f32: tx -= 280.0'f32
    if ty > VH - 110.0'f32: ty -= 110.0'f32
    tx += 14.0'f32
    ty += TOP_H + 14.0'f32
    d = d.rect(tx, ty, w, h, C_TIP, 10.0'f32)
    txt(d, tx + 12.0'f32, ty + 9.0'f32 + 15.0'f32, 15.0'f32, C_TIPINK, l1b, 700)
    txt(d, tx + 12.0'f32 + textW(l1b, 15.0'f32) + 4.0'f32,
        ty + 9.0'f32 + 13.0'f32, 12.0'f32, withA(C_TIPINK, 0.7'f32), l1r)
    var by = ty + 9.0'f32 + 20.0'f32 + 12.0'f32
    for s in l2s:
      txt(d, tx + 12.0'f32, by, 12.0'f32, C_TIPINK, s)
      by += 17.4'f32
    txt(d, tx + 12.0'f32, by, 12.0'f32, C_TIPINK, l3)

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── theme (html:238)
proc applyTheme() =
  ## html:17-20, 106, 244-250. The canvas paint path reads C_* and BG, the dot
  ## field is re-baked; nothing else caches a colour.
  if darkTheme:
    C_BG = 0xFF0B1220'u32; C_PANEL = 0xFF0F1829'u32
    C_PANEL2 = 0xFF142036'u32; C_INK = 0xFFE7EDF6'u32
    C_INK2 = 0xFFA6B3C6'u32; C_INK3 = 0xFF6C7B91'u32
    C_LINE = 0xFF1D2940'u32; C_FOCUS = 0xFF7DB4FF'u32
    C_TIP = 0xFFE7EDF6'u32; C_TIPINK = 0xFF0B1220'u32
    C_bA = 0xFFFF9F1C'u32; C_bT = 0xFFFFBF69'u32
    C_bGrn = 0xFFB49AE0'u32; C_bC = 0xFF8AC926'u32
    BG = [11'i32, 18'i32, 32'i32]
  else:
    C_BG = 0xFFF7F9FBu32; C_PANEL = 0xFFFFFFFF'u32
    C_PANEL2 = 0xFFF2F5F8'u32; C_INK = 0xFF0F1B2Du32
    C_INK2 = 0xFF46566Bu32; C_INK3 = 0xFF8793A4'u32
    C_LINE = 0xFFE2E7EE'u32; C_FOCUS = 0xFF1B6FD8'u32
    C_TIP = 0xFF0F1B2Du32; C_TIPINK = 0xFFFFFFFF'u32
    C_bA = 0xFFE08500'u32; C_bT = 0xFFD9962Fu32
    C_bGrn = 0xFF6A4C93'u32; C_bC = 0xFF5F9A0E'u32
    BG = [247'i32, 249'i32, 251'i32]
  bakeDotField()

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── sequence (html:592)
proc resetMsg() =
  gMsg = "Type A, T, G or C (2 to 40 bases). The partner strand is generated for you."
  gMsgWarn = false

proc cancelReplication() =
  ## html:647-650.
  mode = 0
  mt = 0.0'f32
  for i in 0 ..< MAXN:
    sepA[i] = 0.0'f32
    arrA[i] = 0.0'f32
    arrB[i] = 0.0'f32
  gCap = ""

proc setSeq(s: string; animate: bool) =
  ## html:596-613.
  let old = seqStr
  let prevN = old.len
  seqStr = s
  compStr = ""
  for c in s:
    compStr.add PAIR[baseIdx(c)]
  if animate:
    for i in 0 ..< s.len:
      if i >= prevN or old[i] != s[i]:
        grow[i] = 0.0'f32
  cancelReplication()
  frameGeometry()

proc commitBuf() =
  ## html:615-624 — the input listener, minus the DOM caret bookkeeping.
  if gBuf.len >= 2:
    setSeq(gBuf, true)
    resetMsg()
  else:
    gMsg = "Type at least two bases to build a helix."
    gMsgWarn = true

proc randomSeq() =
  ## html:627-631.
  let L = 18 + int(rnd() * 13.0'f32)
  var s = ""
  for i in 0 ..< L:
    s.add "ATGC"[int(rnd() * 4.0'f32)]
  gBuf = s
  resetMsg()
  setSeq(s, true)

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── replication (html:642)
proc startReplication() =
  ## html:651-665.
  if mode == 0:
    for i in 0 ..< seqStr.len:
      spawnA[i * 3] = (rnd() - 0.5'f32) * 26.0'f32
      spawnA[i * 3 + 1] = 16.0'f32 + rnd() * 20.0'f32
      spawnA[i * 3 + 2] = (rnd() - 0.5'f32) * 34.0'f32
      spawnB[i * 3] = (rnd() - 0.5'f32) * 26.0'f32
      spawnB[i * 3 + 1] = -(16.0'f32 + rnd() * 20.0'f32)
      spawnB[i * 3 + 2] = (rnd() - 0.5'f32) * 34.0'f32
      delA[i] = rnd() * 0.45'f32
      delB[i] = rnd() * 0.45'f32
    mode = 1
    mt = 0.0'f32
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false
    gCap = "Hydrogen bonds break and the strands separate"
  elif mode == 3:
    mode = 4
    mt = 0.0'f32
    gCap = "Returning to the original double helix"

proc stepReplication(dt: float32) =
  ## html:667-693.
  if mode == 0: return
  mt += dt
  let n = seqStr.len
  if mode == 1 or mode == 2 or mode == 3:
    const T1 = 2.6'f32
    let fork = mt / T1 * 1.25'f32
    for i in 0 ..< n:
      sepA[i] = cl((fork - float32(i) / float32(n)) * 3.2'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      let start = 0.55'f32 + (float32(i) / float32(n)) * T1 * 0.95'f32
      arrA[i] = cl((mt - start - delA[i]) / 0.95'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      arrB[i] = cl((mt - start - delB[i]) / 0.95'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
    if mode == 1 and mt > 1.1'f32:
      mode = 2
      gCap = "Free nucleotides pair with each exposed base: A with T, G with C"
    if mode == 2 and mt > 0.55'f32 + T1 * 0.95'f32 + 0.45'f32 + 0.95'f32 + 0.2'f32:
      mode = 3
      gCap = "Two daughter helices, each keeping one original strand: semi-conservative replication"
  elif mode == 4:
    let k1 = min(1.0'f32, mt / 1.1'f32)
    for i in 0 ..< n:
      arrA[i] = min(arrA[i], 1.0'f32 - k1)
      arrB[i] = min(arrB[i], 1.0'f32 - k1)
    if mt > 1.1'f32:
      let f = (mt - 1.1'f32) / 1.6'f32 * 1.25'f32
      for i in 0 ..< n:
        sepA[i] = min(sepA[i], cl((1.25'f32 - f - (1.0'f32 - float32(i) / float32(n))) * 3.2'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))
    if mt > 2.9'f32:
      cancelReplication()

proc tickPlay(dt0: float32) =
  ## html:776-787 — the per-frame integration. repaint() calls this first.
  let dt = min(dt0, 0.05'f32)
  let autoSpeed = if autoRot: 0.42'f32 else: 0.0'f32
  if not dragging:
    spinV += (autoSpeed - spinV) * min(1.0'f32, dt * 1.6'f32)
    spin += spinV * dt
  tilt += (tiltT - tilt) * min(1.0'f32, dt * 6.0'f32)
  zoom += (zoomT - zoom) * min(1.0'f32, dt * 7.0'f32)
  for i in 0 ..< seqStr.len:
    if grow[i] < 1.0'f32:
      grow[i] = min(1.0'f32, grow[i] + dt * 2.4'f32)
  stepReplication(dt)
  annAlpha += ((if mode == 0: 1.0'f32 else: 0.0'f32) - annAlpha) *
              min(1.0'f32, dt * 6.0'f32)
  if gMsgT > 0.0'f32:
    gMsgT -= dt
    if gMsgT <= 0.0'f32:
      resetMsg()

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── picking (html:735)
proc pick(mx, my, cx, cy: float32) =
  ## html:735-756. (mx,my) viz-local, (cx,cy) page.
  if mode != 0:
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false
    return
  var best = -1
  var bd = 1e9'f32
  for i in 0 ..< seqStr.len:
    let x0 = hit[i * 5]
    let y0 = hit[i * 5 + 1]
    let x1 = hit[i * 5 + 2]
    let y1 = hit[i * 5 + 3]
    let dx = x1 - x0
    let dy = y1 - y0
    var L = dx * dx + dy * dy
    if L == 0: L = 1.0'f32
    let t = cl(((mx - x0) * dx + (my - y0) * dy) / L, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
    let dd = sqrt((mx - (x0 + dx * t)) * (mx - (x0 + dx * t)) +
                 (my - (y0 + dy * t)) * (my - (y0 + dy * t))) -
             hit[i * 5 + 4] * 0.25'f32
    if dd < bd:
      bd = dd
      best = i
  let lim = max(10.0'f32, kk * 1.1'f32)
  if best >= 0 and bd < lim:
    hover = best
    hovSeq = best
    gTipI = best
    gTipX = cx
    gTipY = cy - TOP_H
    gTip = true
  else:
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false

proc annRowAt(py: float32): int =
  ## panel hover row for a page y (scroll-adjusted), -1 when none.
  let y = py + gScroll
  for i in 0 .. 4:
    if y >= ANN_PIN_Y[i] - 10.0'f32 and y < ANN_PIN_Y[i] - 10.0'f32 + ANN_HOT_H[i] + 4.0'f32:
      return i
  -1

proc stripIdxAt(px, py: float32): int =
  ## seq-strip char index for a page point, -1 when outside the rows.
  let y = py + gScroll
  if y < SEQ_RA_Y - 14.0'f32 or y > SEQ_RB_Y + 2.0'f32: return -1
  if px < SEQ_ROW_AX or px > 1242.0'f32: return -1
  let i = int((px - SEQ_ROW_AX) / SEQ_ADV)
  if i < 0 or i >= seqStr.len: -1 else: i

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── handlers
proc clickButton(id: int) =
  case id
  of 1:
    darkTheme = not darkTheme
    applyTheme()
  of 2:
    autoRot = not autoRot
  of 3:
    zoomT = max(0.55'f32, zoomT / 1.25'f32)
  of 4:
    zoomT = min(2.8'f32, zoomT * 1.25'f32)
  of 5:
    zoomT = 1.0'f32
    tiltT = 0.16'f32
    spinV = 0.0'f32
  of 6:
    randomSeq()
  of 7:
    startReplication()
  of 8:
    focusSeq = true
    gBuf = seqStr
  else: discard

proc buttonAt(px, py: float32): int =
  ## 1 theme, 2..5 ctrls, 6 random, 7 unzip, 8 seq input, 0 none.
  if px >= 1220.0'f32 and px < 1258.0'f32 and py >= 11.0'f32 and py < 49.0'f32:
    return 1
  if py >= 748.0'f32 and py < 784.0'f32:
    if px >= 34.0'f32 and px < 70.0'f32: return 2
    if px >= 76.0'f32 and px < 112.0'f32: return 3
    if px >= 118.0'f32 and px < 154.0'f32: return 4
    if px >= 160.0'f32 and px < 196.0'f32: return 5
  if px >= VW and py >= TOP_H:
    let y = py + gScroll
    let (ix, iw) = seqInBox()
    if y >= SEQ_ROW_Y and y < SEQ_ROW_Y + 42.0'f32:
      if px >= ix and px < ix + iw: return 8
      if px >= PAN_R - randBtnW() and px < PAN_R: return 6
    if y >= gUnzipY and y < gUnzipY + 46.0'f32 and px >= PAN_L and px < PAN_R:
      return 7
  0

proc blurSeq() =
  if focusSeq:
    focusSeq = false
    if gBuf.len < 2:
      gBuf = seqStr
      resetMsg()

proc onPointerDownInner() =
  let (px, py) = pointerPos()
  pressId = buttonAt(px, py)
  if pressId == 8:
    clickButton(8)
    pressId = 0
  elif pressId == 0:
    blurSeq()
    if px < VW and py >= TOP_H:
      dragging = true
      lastPX = px
      lastPY = py
      velPerp = 0.0'f32
    elif px >= VW and py >= TOP_H:
      scrollDrag = true
      gSLY = py

proc onPointerMoveInner() =
  let (px, py) = pointerPos()
  if dragging:
    ## html:711-718. `landscape` is always true at 1280x816.
    let dx = px - lastPX
    let dy = py - lastPY
    lastPX = px
    lastPY = py
    let perp = dy * cR - dx * sR
    let along = dx * cR + dy * sR
    spin += perp * 0.011'f32
    velPerp = perp * 0.011'f32 * 60.0'f32
    tiltT = cl(tiltT + along * 0.005'f32, -1.05'f32, 1.05'f32)
    return
  if scrollDrag:
    gScroll = cl(gScroll - (py - gSLY), 0.0'f32,
                 max(0.0'f32, gContentBottom - SH))
    gSLY = py
    return
  if px < VW and py >= TOP_H:
    annHot = -1
    pick(px, py - TOP_H, px, py)
  elif px >= VW and py >= TOP_H:
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false
    annHot = annRowAt(py)
    if annHot < 0 and mode == 0:
      ## html:637-640 — hovering the strip highlights the rung.
      let si = stripIdxAt(px, py)
      if si >= 0:
        hover = si
        hovSeq = si
      else:
        hover = -1
        hovSeq = -1
  else:
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false
    annHot = -1

proc onPointerUpInner() =
  let (px, py) = pointerPos()
  if dragging:
    dragging = false
    spinV = velPerp
  scrollDrag = false
  if pressId > 0 and pressId != 8:
    let b = pressId
    pressId = 0
    if buttonAt(px, py) == b:
      clickButton(b)
  else:
    pressId = 0

proc onPointerDown() =
  onPointerDownInner()
  repaint()

proc onPointerMove() =
  onPointerMoveInner()
  repaint()

proc onPointerUp() =
  onPointerUpInner()
  repaint()

proc seqKey(code: int32): bool =
  ## One keyboard event for the focused seq input. Returns true when consumed.
  var ch = '\0'
  if code >= 97 and code <= 122:
    ch = char(code - 32)
  elif code >= 65 and code <= 90:
    ch = char(code)
  if ch == 'A' or ch == 'T' or ch == 'G' or ch == 'C':
    if gBuf.len < MAXN:
      gBuf.add ch
      commitBuf()
    handled()
    return true
  if ch != '\0':
    ## html:620 — invalid letters are left out with a warning.
    gMsg = "Only A, T, G and C are DNA bases — other letters were left out."
    gMsgWarn = true
    gMsgT = 2.6'f32
    handled()
    return true
  if code == keyBackspace or code == keyDelete:
    if gBuf.len > 0:
      gBuf.setLen(gBuf.len - 1)
      commitBuf()
    handled()
    return true
  if code == keyEnter or code == keyEscape:
    blurSeq()
    handled()
    return true
  false

proc onKeyInner() =
  let k = lastKey()
  if k.ctrl or k.alt: return
  if focusSeq:
    discard seqKey(k.code)
    return
  ## html:757-763.
  if k.code == keyLeft:
    spin -= 0.18'f32
    handled()
  elif k.code == keyRight:
    spin += 0.18'f32
    handled()
  elif k.code == keyUp:
    tiltT = max(-1.05'f32, tiltT - 0.12'f32)
    handled()
  elif k.code == keyDown:
    tiltT = min(1.05'f32, tiltT + 0.12'f32)
    handled()
  elif k.code == 43 or k.code == 61:
    zoomT = min(2.8'f32, zoomT * 1.2'f32)
    handled()
  elif k.code == 45 or k.code == 95:
    zoomT = max(0.55'f32, zoomT / 1.2'f32)
    handled()
  elif k.code == keySpace:
    autoRot = not autoRot
    handled()

proc onKey() =
  onKeyInner()
  repaint()

FILE: apps/gallery/039-virtual-gallery.txt
================================================================
TITLE: Galerie Nocturne
FILE: 039-virtual-gallery.html

=== PROMPT (exact prompt used) ===
Build "Galerie Nocturne", a walk-through virtual museum room in CSS 3D. A corridor with a polished marble floor (CSS gradients plus reflection), warm walls (#e9e2d6), soft ceiling spotlights casting pools of light, and eight framed paintings hung on the side walls. Each painting is an original generative artwork rendered on canvas and seeded so it is stable: a colour-field piece, a primary-colour grid, a geometric abstraction, a pointillist sunset, a flow field, an op-art piece, an ink landscape and a gold mosaic. Gilded or slim black frames with museum placards (title, fictional artist, year, medium). Navigation: arrow keys/WASD or on-screen buttons move the camera along the corridor with smooth easing; clicking a painting walks up to it and shows it frontally with a detail card; Escape steps back. A small floor-plan minimap shows your position. Typography: a museum-grade sans for placards and a classic serif for the title. On phones, fall back to a swipeable wall of the same framed works.

SHARED REQUIREMENTS (apply to every page in this 100-page collection):
1. One standalone HTML5 file. All CSS inline in <style>, all JavaScript inline in <script>. No external resources of any kind: no CDN, no libraries, no web fonts, no external images/SVG/audio files, no network requests. All visuals come from CSS, inline SVG, Canvas and procedural code.
2. Valid HTML5: <!DOCTYPE html>, <html lang="en">, <meta charset="utf-8">, a viewport meta tag, a meaningful <title> and meta description, well-nested semantic markup, unique ids, labels/aria attributes for controls, <button type="button"> for actions.
3. Typography from local system font stacks only, always ending in a generic family; display lettering may be drawn with SVG or Canvas.
4. Responsive from 360px phones to large desktops with no horizontal page scroll; canvases match their container and devicePixelRatio (capped at 2) and re-layout on resize.
5. Works offline by opening the file directly (file://); no console errors; storage access guarded with try/catch.
6. Smooth motion via requestAnimationFrame and transform/opacity; animation pauses when the tab is hidden where it matters; honours prefers-reduced-motion with a calmer fallback.
7. Pointer events so mouse and touch both work; visible :focus-visible styles; keyboard support where it makes sense.
8. Any sound is synthesized with the Web Audio API, starts only after a user gesture, and has a visible mute/toggle.
9. Premium finish: a deliberate palette and spacing, polished micro-interactions, a beautiful first frame before any interaction, and original copy (no lorem ipsum). Factual content must be accurate; fictional brands, people and data are clearly fictional.
10. Quality bar: visually impressive, bookmark-worthy, showcase-grade, premium, full of delightful details, and clearly distinct from the other 99 pages in concept, layout, palette, typography and motion.


=== DESCRIPTION ===
Galerie Nocturne is a small museum corridor after closing time, built entirely in CSS 3D. Warm plaster walls (#e9e2d6 where the light falls) stand in darkness, lit only by ceiling track spots. Each spot throws a scallop of light down the wall and a pale beam through the air. A polished black-and-white marble floor reflects the room. Eight original generative paintings hang on alternating walls, each drawn on canvas from a fixed seed so it is identical on every visit: a pointillist harbour sunset, a primary-colour grid, a soft colour-field painting, a bulging op-art checkerboard, an ink-wash mountain hanging scroll, a Bauhaus-style geometric abstraction, a plotter-style flow field, and a gold mosaic sun. Works sit in gilded frames with bead mouldings or in slim black frames with silk or white mats, and each has a crisp museum placard. The titles are set in a Didot/Bodoni-class serif, and the placards and interface in a Helvetica-class sans. All artists, dates and works are fictional.

=== MAJOR VISUAL TECHNIQUES ===
- A true CSS 3D room: walls, floor, ceiling and end wall are planes in one preserve-3d world. A single camera transform (translateZ(perspective) · pitch · yaw · translate) makes it walkable.
- Floor reflections come from real mirrored geometry. A second copy of the walls and paintings sits under a scaleY(-1) group reflected about the floor plane, and a semi-transparent procedural marble texture lets it show through.
- Lighting is layered gradients: per-painting elliptical wall scallops, floor light pools, ceiling fixtures with halos, clip-path light-beam planes that fade out in close-up, a camera-relative depth fog, and a vignette.
- Procedural textures: canvas fBm Perlin marble with veins and grout lines, and a plaster grain tile. Both are exported as data URLs and layered with CSS gradients.
- Eight generative painters on canvas using a seeded PRNG and Perlin noise:
  - divisionist dot sampling with complementary accents
  - recursive grid subdivision with brush texture
  - feathered, layered colour fields
  - supersampled per-pixel lens-warped checkerboard
  - per-column ink-wash gradients with dry-brush texture, pines and a seal
  - multiply-blended geometric shapes
  - a 1,400-line flow field that swirls around a hidden disc
  - gold, lapis and red tesserae laid in concentric rings
- Gilded frames use layered inset shadows and a masked bead ring. A sheen band slides across the gold frames and mosaic as the camera moves.
- Placards and the end-wall title are built at 4× and scaled down inside the 3D context so their text stays sharp up close.
- The camera uses exponential easing with speed caps, subtle head-bob, idle sway and pointer parallax.

=== INTERACTION MODEL ===
- ↑/W and ↓/S walk the corridor; hold for continuous walking. ←/A and →/D turn to look at either wall. The mouse wheel and a vertical drag also walk, and a horizontal drag turns.
- Clicking a painting walks up to it and turns to face it, framing the work and its placard beside a detail card with title, fictional artist, year, medium, size, a curatorial note and the seed. Prev/Next or ←/→ glide to neighbouring works, even across the corridor. Escape, Step back or ↓ return to the centre line.
- Clicking the floor walks to that spot. Clicking a wall approaches the nearest work on it. Clicking the end wall walks to the end of the room.
- Enter or the View button approaches the work you are facing. The top-right label always names the nearest work, and keys 1–8 jump straight to a work.
- An on-screen pad has hold-to-walk and turn buttons for touch and mouse. Keyboard activation takes discrete steps.
- The floor-plan minimap shows the corridor, colour-coded painting positions, and your position with a view cone. Its marks and the I–VIII index buttons are clickable.
- On phones (≤720 px) the page switches to a swipeable, scroll-snapped wall of the same framed works under spotlights, with placards, dots and prev/next buttons. Tapping a work opens a bottom sheet with a larger image and the full card.
- Reduced motion makes camera moves near-instant and removes the bob, sway and fades.

const DPR = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
const ROMAN = ['I', 'II', 'III', 'IV', 'V', 'VI', 'VII', 'VIII'];
const URLF = s => 'url("' + s + '")';

/* ============================================================ seeded randomness + noise */
function mulberry32(a){ return () => { a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0; let t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a); t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t; return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }
function makeNoise(rand){
  const perm = new Uint8Array(512), base = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) base.push(i);
  for (let i = 255; i > 0; i--){ const j = Math.floor(rand() * (i + 1)); const t = base[i]; base[i] = base[j]; base[j] = t; }
  for (let i = 0; i < 512; i++) perm[i] = base[i & 255];
  const grad = (h, x, y) => { switch (h & 7){ case 0: return x + y; case 1: return -x + y; case 2: return x - y; case 3: return -x - y; case 4: return x; case 5: return -x; case 6: return y; default: return -y; } };
  const fade = t => t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
  return (x, y) => {
    const xf = Math.floor(x), yf = Math.floor(y), X = xf & 255, Y = yf & 255;
    x -= xf; y -= yf;
    const u = fade(x), v = fade(y);
    const aa = perm[perm[X] + Y], ab = perm[perm[X] + Y + 1], ba = perm[perm[X + 1] + Y], bb = perm[perm[X + 1] + Y + 1];
    const g1 = grad(aa, x, y), g2 = grad(ba, x - 1, y), g3 = grad(ab, x, y - 1), g4 = grad(bb, x - 1, y - 1);
    const l1 = g1 + u * (g2 - g1), l2 = g3 + u * (g4 - g3);
    return (l1 + v * (l2 - l1)) * .7;
  };
}
function fbm(N, x, y, oct){ let s = 0, a = .5, f = 1; for (let i = 0; i < oct; i++){ s += a * N(x * f, y * f); f *= 2.03; a *= .5; } return s; }
const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
function grainPass(ctx, W, H, amt, R){
  const img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, W, H), d = img.data;
  for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4){ const n = (R() - .5) * amt; d[i] += n; d[i + 1] += n; d[i + 2] += n; }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
}

/* ============================================================ the eight generative works */
function genSunset(ctx, W, H, R, N){
  const hz = H * .57, sx = W * .63, sy = hz - H * .11, sr = W * .052;
  let g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, hz);
  g.addColorStop(0, '#2f2d5e'); g.addColorStop(.42, '#8a5487'); g.addColorStop(.78, '#e3815a'); g.addColorStop(1, '#f5bf6c');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, hz);
  g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, hz, 0, H);
  g.addColorStop(0, '#b86d6b'); g.addColorStop(.35, '#4f4776'); g.addColorStop(1, '#1d2244');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, hz, W, H - hz);
  const landTop = x => { const t = x / W; if (t > .47) return 1e9; return hz - H * .21 * Math.pow(1 - t / .47, 1.25) - H * .025 * N(t * 7, 1.3); };
  const tx0 = W * .115, tx1 = W * .135, tTop = landTop(W * .125) - H * .13;
  const boat = { x: W * .34, y: hz + H * .1 };
  function col(x, y){
    const lt = landTop(x);
    if (x > tx0 && x < tx1 && y > tTop && y < lt + 3) return [262, 28, 22];
    if (Math.hypot(x - (tx0 + tx1) / 2, y - (tTop - H * .014)) < W * .012) return [52, 100, 84];
    if (y > lt && y < hz + H * .014) return [275 + R() * 30, 30, 15 + R() * 9];
    const bx = x - boat.x, by = boat.y - y;
    if (by > 0 && by < H * .08 && bx > -by * .55 && bx < 2) return [282, 32, 17];
    if (by > -H * .01 && by <= 0 && Math.abs(bx + W * .005) < W * .032) return [282, 32, 14];
    const ds = Math.hypot(x - sx, y - sy);
    if (y < hz){
      if (ds < sr) return [50, 100, 86 + R() * 8];
      const t = y / hz;
      let h = t < .45 ? lerp(245, 305, t / .45) : t < .8 ? lerp(305, 370, (t - .45) / .35) : lerp(10, 44, (t - .8) / .2);
      let l = lerp(30, 68, t), s = lerp(45, 82, t);
      const glow = Math.max(0, 1 - ds / (sr * 5.5));
      l += glow * 22; h = lerp(h, 45, glow * .7);
      if (N(x / W * 3, y / H * 15) > .2 && t > .25 && t < .9){ h = 330 + (R() - .5) * 24; l -= 9; s -= 8; }
      return [h, s, l];
    }
    const t = (y - hz) / (H - hz);
    let h = lerp(250, 236, t), l = lerp(46, 22, t), s = 44;
    const wv = Math.sin(y * .45 * (1 + t) * (330 / W) + N(x * .02 * (330 / W), y * .05) * 5);
    l += wv * 5;
    const cw = W * (.035 + t * .12) * (.7 + .3 * N(y * .08, 2.1));
    if (Math.abs(x - sx) < cw && wv > -.4){ h = 36 + R() * 12; s = 92; l = lerp(78, 52, t) + wv * 6; }
    if (t < .07){ h = lerp(22, h, t / .07); l += (1 - t / .07) * 12; }
    return [h, s, l];
  }
  const n = Math.round(W * H / 26), rad = W / 290;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++){
    const x = R() * W, y = R() * H;
    let [h, s, l] = col(x, y);
    h += (R() - .5) * 28; l += (R() - .5) * 14;
    const r = R();
    if (r < .08){ h += 180; s *= .8; } else if (r < .2){ h += R() < .5 ? -38 : 38; }
    ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${h.toFixed(0)},${Math.min(100, s).toFixed(0)}%,${Math.max(4, Math.min(95, l)).toFixed(0)}%)`;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, rad * (.75 + R() * .7), 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 420; i++){
    const a = R() * 6.2832, rr = Math.sqrt(R()) * sr * 1.05;
    ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${(46 + R() * 12).toFixed(0)},100%,${(80 + R() * 14).toFixed(0)}%)`;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx + Math.cos(a) * rr, sy + Math.sin(a) * rr, rad * .8, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  }
}

function genGrid(ctx, W, H, R){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#efeadf'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const leaves = [], lines = [], minS = W * .13;
  (function split(r, d){
    const canV = r.w > minS * 2, canH = r.h > minS * 2;
    if (d > 4 || (!canV && !canH) || (d > 1 && R() < .2)){ leaves.push(r); return; }
    const vert = canV && (!canH || (r.w >= r.h ? R() < .7 : R() < .3));
    const f = [.3, .38, .5, .62, .7][Math.floor(R() * 5)];
    if (vert){
      const x = r.x + Math.max(minS, Math.min(r.w - minS, r.w * f));
      lines.push([x, r.y, x, r.y + r.h]);
      split({ x: r.x, y: r.y, w: x - r.x, h: r.h }, d + 1); split({ x, y: r.y, w: r.x + r.w - x, h: r.h }, d + 1);
    } else {
      const y = r.y + Math.max(minS, Math.min(r.h - minS, r.h * f));
      lines.push([r.x, y, r.x + r.w, y]);
      split({ x: r.x, y: r.y, w: r.w, h: y - r.y }, d + 1); split({ x: r.x, y, w: r.w, h: r.y + r.h - y }, d + 1);
    }
  })({ x: 0, y: 0, w: W, h: H }, 0);
  leaves.sort((a, b) => b.w * b.h - a.w * a.h);
  const fill = new Map();
  const whites = ['#efeadf', '#ebe5d8', '#f2eee4'];
  leaves.forEach(l => fill.set(l, whites[Math.floor(R() * 3)]));
  fill.set(leaves[0], '#cf2a1f');
  const mid = leaves.slice(2, Math.max(3, Math.floor(leaves.length * .5)));
  const small = leaves.slice(Math.floor(leaves.length * .5));
  if (mid.length) fill.set(mid[Math.floor(R() * mid.length)], '#1f3f96');
  if (small.length) fill.set(small[Math.floor(R() * small.length)], '#f0c02c');
  if (small.length > 2) fill.set(small[Math.floor(R() * small.length)], R() < .5 ? '#f0c02c' : '#161616');
  for (const l of leaves){
    ctx.fillStyle = fill.get(l); ctx.fillRect(l.x, l.y, l.w, l.h);
    ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(l.x, l.y, l.w, l.h); ctx.clip();
    const horiz = l.w > l.h;
    for (let k = 0; k < 70; k++){
      ctx.globalAlpha = .05 + R() * .06;
      ctx.fillStyle = R() < .5 ? '#ffffff' : '#000000';
      if (horiz) ctx.fillRect(l.x + R() * l.w * .3 - l.w * .1, l.y + R() * l.h, l.w * (.4 + R() * .7), .6 + R() * 1.6);
      else ctx.fillRect(l.x + R() * l.w, l.y + R() * l.h * .3 - l.h * .1, .6 + R() * 1.6, l.h * (.4 + R() * .7));
    }
    ctx.restore(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#141414'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .024; ctx.lineCap = 'square';
  for (const [x1, y1, x2, y2] of lines){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x1, y1); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke(); }
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 10, R);
}

function genColorField(ctx, W, H, R, N){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#3a0f18'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++){ ctx.fillStyle = R() < .5 ? 'rgba(96,22,34,.06)' : 'rgba(18,3,8,.07)'; ctx.fillRect(0, R() * H, W, R() * H * .07); }
  const blocks = [
    { y0: .055, y1: .52, a: [210, 62, 38], b: [238, 124, 60] },
    { y0: .57, y1: .612, a: [36, 8, 16], b: [60, 16, 27] },
    { y0: .66, y1: .945, a: [86, 28, 64], b: [124, 50, 88] }
  ];
  const x0 = W * .08, x1 = W * .92;
  for (const bl of blocks){
    const Y0 = H * bl.y0, Y1 = H * bl.y1, j = W * .014;
    for (let k = 0; k < 48; k++){
      const t = R();
      const c = bl.a.map((v, i) => Math.round(v + (bl.b[i] - v) * t));
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${c},.075)`;
      const a0 = x0 + (R() - .5) * j * 2, a1 = x1 + (R() - .5) * j * 2, b0 = Y0 + (R() - .5) * j * 2, b1 = Y1 + (R() - .5) * j * 2;
      ctx.fillRect(a0, b0, a1 - a0, b1 - b0);
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < 280; k++){
      const y = Y0 + R() * (Y1 - Y0), t = R();
      const c = bl.b.map(v => Math.min(255, Math.round(v * (.8 + t * .45))));
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${c},${(.035 + R() * .05).toFixed(3)})`; ctx.lineWidth = 1 + R() * W * .012;
      ctx.beginPath(); const xs = x0 + R() * W * .08; ctx.moveTo(xs, y);
      const len = W * (.2 + R() * .62);
      for (let s = 0; s < len; s += W * .02) ctx.lineTo(xs + s, y + N((xs + s) * .012, y * .012) * H * .012);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  const rg = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * .5, H * .28, 0, W * .5, H * .28, W * .6);
  rg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,176,96,.2)'); rg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,176,96,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = rg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 12, R);
}

function genOp(ctx, W, H, R){
  const img = ctx.createImageData(W, H), d = img.data;
  const cells = 15, cs = W / cells;
  const cx = W * (.56 + (R() - .5) * .05), cy = H * (.44 + (R() - .5) * .05), R0 = W * .43;
  const A0 = 242, A1 = 238, A2 = 229, B0 = 17, B1 = 16, B2 = 20;
  const inv = 1 / R0;
  for (let y = 0; y < H; y++){
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++){
      let s = 0;
      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++){
        const px = x + (k & 1 ? .75 : .25), py = y + (k & 2 ? .75 : .25);
        const dx = px - cx, dy = py - cy, r2 = (dx * dx + dy * dy) * inv * inv;
        let m = 1; if (r2 < 1){ const q = 1 - r2; m = 1 - .56 * q * q; }
        s += (Math.floor((cx + dx * m) / cs) + Math.floor((cy + dy * m * 1.04) / cs)) & 1;
      }
      const t = s / 4, i = (y * W + x) * 4;
      d[i] = A0 + (B0 - A0) * t; d[i + 1] = A1 + (B1 - A1) * t; d[i + 2] = A2 + (B2 - A2) * t; d[i + 3] = 255;
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 7, R);
}

function genInk(ctx, W, H, R, N){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#efe6d2'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  let pg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
  pg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(140,100,50,.04)'); pg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(140,100,50,.12)');
  ctx.fillStyle = pg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,96,60,.07)'; ctx.lineWidth = .6;
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++){ const x = R() * W, y = R() * H; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x + (R() - .5) * W * .2, y + (R() - .5) * 6); ctx.stroke(); }
  const layers = 5;
  const inkAt = (t, a) => { const v = Math.round(22 + (1 - t) * 52); return `rgba(${v},${v},${v + 8},${a})`; };
  let nearRidge = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < layers; i++){
    const t = i / (layers - 1);
    const base = H * (.2 + t * .34), amp = H * (.09 + t * .05), len = H * (.07 + t * .09), a = .12 + t * .74;
    const peaks = []; for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) peaks.push({ x: R() * W, w: W * (.1 + R() * .22), h: .5 + R() * .7 });
    const ridge = x => { let v = .6 * N(x / W * 2.4 + i * 3.7, i * 1.3) + .3 * N(x / W * 7 + i, 5 + i); for (const p of peaks) v += p.h * Math.exp(-(((x - p.x) / p.w) ** 2)); return base - amp * v; };
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++){
      const top = ridge(x) + (R() - .5) * 1.3;
      const L = len * (.65 + .35 * N(x * .03, i * 9));
      const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, top, 0, top + L);
      g.addColorStop(0, inkAt(t, a)); g.addColorStop(.18, inkAt(t, a * .62)); g.addColorStop(1, inkAt(t, 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(x, top, 1.3, L);
    }
    if (t > .3){
      ctx.strokeStyle = inkAt(t, .28 * t); ctx.lineWidth = W * .004;
      for (let k = 0; k < W * .35 * t; k++){
        const x = R() * W, y = ridge(x) + R() * len * .55;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x + W * (.01 + R() * .02) * (R() < .5 ? -1 : 1), y + H * (.008 + R() * .012)); ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
    const mg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, base + len * .2, 0, base + len * 1.3);
    mg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(239,230,210,0)'); mg.addColorStop(.5, 'rgba(239,230,210,.7)'); mg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(239,230,210,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = mg; ctx.fillRect(0, base + len * .2, W, len * 1.1);
    if (i === layers - 1) nearRidge = ridge;
  }
  // foreground rock with pines
  const rockTop = x => H * .8 - H * .1 * Math.exp(-(((x - W * .18) / (W * .3)) ** 2)) - H * .02 * N(x * .02, 40);
  for (let x = 0; x < W * .62; x++){
    const top = rockTop(x), L = H * .16 * (1 - x / (W * .62));
    if (L <= 0) continue;
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, top, 0, top + L);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(20,20,26,.92)'); g.addColorStop(.3, 'rgba(20,20,26,.5)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(20,20,26,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(x, top, 1.3, L);
  }
  const pine = (x, y, h, lean) => {
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(16,16,20,.9)'; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineWidth = W * .012; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + lean * .6, y - h * .5, x + lean, y - h); ctx.stroke();
    for (let k = 0; k < 7; k++){
      const f = .3 + k * .1, bx = x + lean * f * f, by = y - h * f, side = k % 2 ? 1 : -1, span = W * (.1 - k * .009);
      for (let s = 0; s < 16; s++){
        const px = bx + side * span * (s / 16), py = by - Math.sin(s / 16 * 3.1) * H * .006;
        ctx.lineWidth = W * .004; ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(16,16,20,${(.55 + R() * .35).toFixed(2)})`;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(px, py); ctx.lineTo(px + (R() - .5) * W * .025, py - H * (.006 + R() * .008)); ctx.stroke();
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(px, py); ctx.lineTo(px + (R() - .5) * W * .025, py + H * (.004 + R() * .006)); ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
  };
  pine(W * .2, rockTop(W * .2) + 2, H * .22, W * .06);
  pine(W * .34, rockTop(W * .34) + 2, H * .14, -W * .03);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(20,20,26,.8)'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .005;
  for (let b = 0; b < 3; b++){
    const bx = W * (.62 + b * .07), by = H * (.1 + b * .018), s = W * .022;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(bx - s, by - s * .2); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(bx - s * .4, by - s * .5, bx, by); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(bx + s * .4, by - s * .5, bx + s, by - s * .25); ctx.stroke();
  }
  const sz = W * .1, sxp = W * .78, syp = H * .9;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#b3302a'; ctx.fillRect(sxp, syp, sz, sz * 1.1);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(245,230,210,.85)'; ctx.lineWidth = sz * .07;
  ctx.strokeRect(sxp + sz * .16, syp + sz * .16, sz * .68, sz * .78);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sxp + sz * .5, syp + sz * .22); ctx.lineTo(sxp + sz * .5, syp + sz * .88); ctx.moveTo(sxp + sz * .24, syp + sz * .55); ctx.lineTo(sxp + sz * .76, syp + sz * .55); ctx.stroke();
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 8, R);
}

function genGeometric(ctx, W, H, R){
  const j = v => v + (R() - .5) * W * .02;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#eee2c7'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const vg = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * .45, H * .45, 0, W * .5, H * .5, W * .7);
  vg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,250,235,.3)'); vg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(120,90,50,.18)');
  ctx.fillStyle = vg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const cx = j(W * .33), cy = j(H * .47), r = H * .31;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(240,206,120,.45)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * 1.3, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#1b1b1b'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .003;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W * .62, H * 1.1, H * .95, Math.PI * 1.12, Math.PI * 1.72); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#23407a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#e2b13a'; ctx.lineWidth = H * .032; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * .6, 0, 6.2832); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#141414'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * .2, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#c63b2b';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(j(W * .5), H * .9); ctx.lineTo(j(W * .76), H * .14); ctx.lineTo(j(W * .93), H * .82); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#d58f2e'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(j(W * .83), H * .3, H * .12, Math.PI * .1, Math.PI * 1.1); ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#151515'; ctx.lineWidth = H * .034; ctx.lineCap = 'butt';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(W * .06, H * .8); ctx.lineTo(W * .7, H * .08); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.lineWidth = W * .0035;
  for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(W * (.58 + k * .018), H * .06); ctx.lineTo(W * (.98), H * (.36 + k * .045)); ctx.stroke(); }
  const q = W * .026;
  for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++){ ctx.fillStyle = k % 2 ? '#eee2c7' : '#171717'; ctx.fillRect(W * .08 + k * q, H * .88, q, q); ctx.fillStyle = k % 2 ? '#171717' : '#eee2c7'; ctx.fillRect(W * .08 + k * q, H * .88 + q, q, q); }
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#171717'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .002; ctx.strokeRect(W * .08, H * .88, q * 10, q * 2);
  const dots = [['#c63b2b', .56, .52, .028], ['#141414', .62, .66, .018], ['#23407a', .9, .12, .022], ['#e2b13a', .12, .16, .03]];
  for (const [c, x, y, rr] of dots){ ctx.fillStyle = c; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(j(W * x), j(H * y), W * rr, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#141414'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .004;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W * .66, H * .6, W * .06, 0, 6.2832); ctx.stroke();
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 14, R);
}

function genFlow(ctx, W, H, R, N){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#efe8da'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const C = { x: W * .55, y: H * .42, r: W * .27 };
  const outer = ['#173f4f', '#1c2733', '#2f6a73', '#0f1b24', '#24555f'];
  const inner = ['#b4502f', '#cf7440', '#8e3522', '#c05a33'];
  const sc = 2.2 / W, step = W / 320;
  const count = Math.round(1400 * (W * H) / (544 * 704));
  ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++){
    let x = R() * W, y = R() * H;
    const inside = Math.hypot(x - C.x, y - C.y) < C.r;
    const pal = inside ? inner : outer;
    ctx.strokeStyle = R() < .05 ? '#c69a3c' : pal[Math.floor(R() * pal.length)];
    ctx.globalAlpha = .5 + R() * .4;
    ctx.lineWidth = W / 700 * (.6 + R() * 1.4);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y);
    const steps = 30 + R() * 70;
    for (let s = 0; s < steps; s++){
      let ang = N(x * sc, y * sc) * Math.PI * 2.6;
      const dx = x - C.x, dy = y - C.y, dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      const w = Math.exp(-(((dist - C.r) / (C.r * .45)) ** 2));
      const tang = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + Math.PI / 2;
      ang = ang * (1 - w) + tang * w;
      x += Math.cos(ang) * step; y += Math.sin(ang) * step;
      if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x > W || y > H) break;
      ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 8, R);
}

function genMosaic(ctx, W, H, R){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#2a2017'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const t = W / 36, cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2, discR = W * .3;
  const tile = (x, y, s, ang, kind) => {
    let base;
    if (kind === 'gold'){ const l = 42 + R() * 30; base = [`hsl(${(40 + R() * 9).toFixed(0)},${(62 + R() * 20).toFixed(0)}%,${(l + 14).toFixed(0)}%)`, `hsl(${(38 + R() * 8).toFixed(0)},70%,${(l - 12).toFixed(0)}%)`]; if (R() < .06) base[0] = '#fff3c4'; }
    else if (kind === 'lapis'){ const l = 26 + R() * 14; base = [`hsl(${(218 + R() * 10).toFixed(0)},62%,${(l + 10).toFixed(0)}%)`, `hsl(222,66%,${l.toFixed(0)}%)`]; }
    else if (kind === 'red'){ const l = 28 + R() * 10; base = [`hsl(${(356 + R() * 8).toFixed(0)},68%,${(l + 10).toFixed(0)}%)`, `hsl(358,70%,${l.toFixed(0)}%)`]; }
    else { const l = 84 + R() * 10; base = [`hsl(44,40%,${l.toFixed(0)}%)`, `hsl(42,30%,${(l - 14).toFixed(0)}%)`]; }
    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(ang + (R() - .5) * .12);
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(-s / 2, -s / 2, s / 2, s / 2);
    g.addColorStop(0, base[0]); g.addColorStop(1, base[1]);
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    const w = s * (.86 + R() * .1), h = s * (.84 + R() * .1);
    ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h);
    ctx.restore();
  };
  for (let row = 0, y = t / 2; y < H + t; y += t, row++){
    const off = (row % 2) * t * .5;
    for (let x = t / 2 - off; x < W + t; x += t){
      if (Math.hypot(x - cx, y - cy) < discR + t * 1.6) continue;
      tile(x + (R() - .5) * t * .1, y + (R() - .5) * t * .1, t, 0, 'gold');
    }
  }
  for (let ring = 0, r = t * .55; r < discR + t * 1.6; r += t * .98, ring++){
    const n = Math.max(1, Math.round(6.2832 * r / t));
    const ph = R() * 6.2832;
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++){
      const a = ph + k / n * 6.2832, x = cx + Math.cos(a) * r, y = cy + Math.sin(a) * r;
      let kind = 'gold';
      const rel = r / discR;
      const ray = Math.floor(((a % 6.2832) + 6.2832) % 6.2832 / (6.2832 / 16)) % 2;
      if (rel < .22) kind = ring === 0 ? 'white' : 'gold';
      else if (rel < .34) kind = 'red';
      else if (rel < .48) kind = 'gold';
      else if (rel < .8) kind = ray ? 'lapis' : 'gold';
      else if (rel < .92) kind = 'red';
      else if (rel < 1.02) kind = 'lapis';
      else kind = 'gold';
      tile(x, y, t * .98, a, kind);
    }
  }
  const sh = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * .3, H * .25, 0, W * .3, H * .25, W * .8);
  sh.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,240,200,.14)'); sh.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,.18)');
  ctx.fillStyle = sh; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
}
