    scX[cnt] = if side == 'L': G_ZNEAR - w.z else: w.z - G_ZFAR
    scY[cnt] = (w.cy - G_CEILY) - 34.0'f32
    scA[cnt] = float32(rnd32(w.w * 0.95'f32 + 70.0'f32))
    scB[cnt] = float32(rnd32(w.h * 0.9'f32 + 150.0'f32))
    inc cnt

  for y in 0 ..< TH_L:
    let fy = float32(y) / float32(TH_L)
    let base = csample([
      (0.0'f32, hex(0x3F372D)), (0.40'f32, hex(0x53493C)),
      (1.0'f32, hex(0x4A4135))
    ], fy)
    let depth = float32(TH_L - y)
    for x in 0 ..< TW_L:
      var p = Px()
      ov(p, base)
      let ti = ((y mod 128) * 128 + (x mod 128)) * 4
      p.over(float32(plas[ti]) / 255.0'f32, float32(plas[ti + 1]) / 255.0'f32,
             float32(plas[ti + 2]) / 255.0'f32, float32(plas[ti + 3]) / 255.0'f32)
      if depth < 13.0'f32:
        ov(p, hex(0x17130F))
      elif depth < 15.0'f32:
        ov(p, hex(0x4A4035))
      elif depth < 70.0'f32:
        p.over(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.45'f32 * (1.0'f32 - (depth - 15.0'f32) / 55.0'f32))
      if float32(y) < 34.0'f32:
        p.over(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.6'f32 * (1.0'f32 - float32(y) / 34.0'f32))
      storePx(result, int32((y * TW_L + x) * 4), p)

  for k in 0 ..< cnt:
    let by0 = max(0'i32, int32(floor(scY[k] - scB[k])))
    let by1 = min(int32(TH_L) - 1, int32(ceil(scY[k] + scB[k])))
    let bx0 = max(0'i32, int32(floor(scX[k] - scA[k])))
    let bx1 = min(int32(TW_L) - 1, int32(ceil(scX[k] + scA[k])))
    for y2 in by0 .. by1:
      for x2 in bx0 .. bx1:
        let dx = (float32(x2) + 0.5'f32 - scX[k]) / scA[k]
        let dy = (float32(y2) + 0.5'f32 - scY[k]) / scB[k]
        let t = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
        if t <= 1.0'f32:
          let idx = (y2 * TW_L + x2) * 4
          var p = loadPx(result, idx)
          ov(p, csample([
            (0.0'f32, rgba(255, 243, 220, 0.97'f32)),
            (0.30'f32, rgba(246, 232, 206, 0.72'f32)),
            (0.58'f32, rgba(233, 226, 214, 0.28'f32)),
            (0.76'f32, rgba(233, 226, 214, 0.0'f32))
          ], t))
          storePx(result, idx, p)

proc floorTex(marb: seq[byte]): seq[byte] =
  result = newSeq[byte](TW_F * TH_F * 4)
  for y in 0 ..< TH_F:
    let cy = (y mod 128) * 2
    for x in 0 ..< TW_F:
      var p = Px()
      let cx = (x mod 128) * 2
      var mr, mg, mb: float32
      for k in 0 .. 3:
        let mi = ((cy + (k shr 1)) * 256 + (cx + (k and 1))) * 4
        mr += float32(marb[mi])
        mg += float32(marb[mi + 1])
        mb += float32(marb[mi + 2])
      p.over(mr / 1020.0'f32, mg / 1020.0'f32, mb / 1020.0'f32, 188.0'f32 / 255.0'f32)
      let fx = float32(x) / float32(TW_F)
      if fx < 0.09'f32:
        p.over(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.55'f32 * (1.0'f32 - fx / 0.09'f32))
      elif fx > 0.91'f32:
        p.over(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.55'f32 * ((fx - 0.91'f32) / 0.09'f32))
      storePx(result, int32((y * TW_F + x) * 4), p)

  for k in 0 ..< 8:
    let w = WORKS[k]
    let lx = if w.side == 'L': 78.0'f32 else: 522.0'f32
    let ly = w.z - G_ZFAR
    let y0 = max(0'i32, int32(ly) - 200)
    let y1 = min(int32(TH_F), int32(ly) + 200)
    let x0 = max(0'i32, int32(lx) - 150)
    let x1 = min(int32(TW_F), int32(lx) + 150)
    for y in y0 ..< y1:
      for x in x0 ..< x1:
        let dx = (float32(x) + 0.5'f32 - lx) / 150.0'f32
        let dy = (float32(y) + 0.5'f32 - ly) / 200.0'f32
        let t = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
        if t <= 1.0'f32:
          let idx = (y * TW_F + x) * 4
          var p = loadPx(result, idx)
          ov(p, csample([
            (0.0'f32, rgba(255, 236, 200, 0.30'f32)),
            (0.45'f32, rgba(255, 236, 200, 0.10'f32)),
            (0.72'f32, rgba(255, 236, 200, 0.0'f32))
          ], t))
          storePx(result, idx, p)

  for y in 0 ..< 330:
    for x in 40 ..< 560:
      let dx = (float32(x) + 0.5'f32 - 300.0'f32) / 260.0'f32
      let dy = (float32(y) + 0.5'f32 - 90.0'f32) / 240.0'f32
      let t = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
      if t <= 1.0'f32:
        let idx = int32((y * TW_F + x) * 4)
        var p = loadPx(result, idx)
        ov(p, csample([
          (0.0'f32, rgba(255, 236, 200, 0.18'f32)),
          (0.75'f32, rgba(255, 236, 200, 0.0'f32))
        ], t))
        storePx(result, idx, p)

proc ceilTex(): seq[byte] =
  result = newSeq[byte](TW_F * TH_F * 4)
  const CEIL = [
    (0.0'f32, hex(0x100E0C)),
    (0.5'f32, hex(0x1D1A16)),
    (1.0'f32, hex(0x100E0C))
  ]
  for y in 0 ..< TH_F:
    for x in 0 ..< TW_F:
      let fx = float32(x) / float32(TW_F)
      let t = if fx < 0.2'f32: fx / 0.2'f32 * 0.5'f32
              elif fx < 0.8'f32: 0.5'f32
              else: 0.5'f32 + (1.0'f32 - fx) / 0.2'f32 * 0.5'f32
      var p = Px(r: 0.0'f32, g: 0.0'f32, b: 0.0'f32, a: 1.0'f32)
      ov(p, csample(CEIL, t))
      ## Recessed ceiling light tracks at pixel columns 124..132 and 468..476 (html:832).
      if (x >= 124 and x < 132) or (x >= 468 and x < 476):
        ov(p, hex(0x0B0A08))
      storePx(result, int32((y * TW_F + x) * 4), p)

  for k in 0 ..< 8:
    let w = WORKS[k]
    let lx = if w.side == 'L': 128.0'f32 else: 472.0'f32
    let ly = G_ZNEAR - w.z
    for y in max(0'i32, int32(ly) - 90) ..< min(int32(TH_F), int32(ly) + 90):
      for x in max(0'i32, int32(lx) - 60) ..< min(int32(TW_F), int32(lx) + 60):
        let dx = (float32(x) + 0.5'f32 - lx) / 60.0'f32
        let dy = (float32(y) + 0.5'f32 - ly) / 90.0'f32
        let t = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
        if t < 0.70'f32:
          let idx = (y * TW_F + x) * 4
          var p = loadPx(result, idx)
          p.a = 1.0'f32
          p.over(1.0'f32, 230.0'f32 / 255.0'f32, 180.0'f32 / 255.0'f32,
                 0.16'f32 * (1.0'f32 - t / 0.70'f32))
          storePx(result, idx, p)
    for y in max(0'i32, int32(ly) - 9) ..< min(int32(TH_F), int32(ly) + 9):
      for x in max(0'i32, int32(lx) - 9) ..< min(int32(TW_F), int32(lx) + 9):
        let dx = float32(x) + 0.5'f32 - lx
        let dy = float32(y) + 0.5'f32 - ly
        let rad = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
        var c: uint32
        if rad <= 3.0'f32:
          c = hex(0xFFF6E0)
        elif rad >= 4.0'f32 and rad <= 9.0'f32:
          c = rgba(255, 230, 180, 0.9'f32 * (1.0'f32 - (rad - 4.0'f32) / 5.0'f32))
        else:
          continue
        let idx = (y * TW_F + x) * 4
        var p = loadPx(result, idx)
        p.a = 1.0'f32
        ov(p, c)
        storePx(result, idx, p)

proc endTex(): seq[byte] =
  result = newSeq[byte](TW_E * TH_E * 4)
  const ENDG = [(0.0'f32, hex(0x3F372D)), (1.0'f32, hex(0x4F463A))]
  for y in 0 ..< TH_E:
    let fy = float32(y) / float32(TH_E)
    let depth = float32(TH_E - y)
    for x in 0 ..< TW_E:
      var p = Px(r: 0.0'f32, g: 0.0'f32, b: 0.0'f32, a: 1.0'f32)
      ov(p, csample(ENDG, fy))
      if depth < 13.0'f32: ov(p, hex(0x17130F))
      elif depth < 15.0'f32: ov(p, hex(0x4A4035))
      if float32(y) < 34.0'f32:
        p.over(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.55'f32 * (1.0'f32 - float32(y) / 34.0'f32))
      let dx = (float32(x) + 0.5'f32 - 300.0'f32) / 280.0'f32
      let dy = (float32(y) + 0.5'f32 - 150.0'f32) / 330.0'f32
      let t = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
      if t <= 1.0'f32:
        ov(p, csample([
          (0.0'f32, rgba(255, 243, 220, 0.82'f32)),
          (0.42'f32, rgba(240, 228, 206, 0.45'f32)),
          (0.78'f32, rgba(233, 226, 214, 0.0'f32))
        ], t))
      storePx(result, int32((y * TW_E + x) * 4), p)

# ───────────── 10. 3D Projection, Ray-Caster & Perspective Quad Rasteriser
type
  Cam = object
    x, y, z, rot: float32

  Xf = object
    ca, sa, cb, sb: float32
    ox, oy, oz: float32
    persp: float32
    mir: bool

  Quad = object
    v: array[4, array[6, float32]]
    n: int

const
  F_WALLL = 0'i32
  F_WALLR = 1'i32
  F_END   = 2'i32
  F_FLOOR = 3'i32
  F_CEIL  = 4'i32
  QPX_MAXSUB = 4'i32

  BEAM_W  = 150.0'f32
  BEAM_H  = 420.0'f32
  BEAM_A  = 0.15'f32
  BEAM_B  = 0.05'f32
  BEAM_C  = 0.02'f32
  BEAM_CR = 255.0'f32
  BEAM_CG = 238.0'f32
  BEAM_CB = 205.0'f32

var
  cam = Cam(z: 160.0'f32)
  tgt = Cam(z: 160.0'f32)
  mode = 0
  current = -1'i32
  bob = 0.0'f32
  idleT = 0.0'f32
  lookX, lookY = 0.0'f32
  lookTX, lookTY = 0.0'f32
  pitch, tgtPitch = 0.0'f32
  dragOn = false
  dragMoved = false
  dragX, dragY = 0.0'f32
  dragRot, dragPitch = 0.0'f32
  cardT = -1.0'f32
  veilT = 1.0'f32
  appT = 0.0'f32
  sheenPos = 50.0'f32
  sceneReady = false
  frameDtPub = 0.0'f32

  corrBuf: seq[byte]
  stageBgBuf: seq[byte]
  fogUnitA: seq[float32]
  vigUnitA: seq[float32]
  dbuf, mbuf: seq[float32]
  spritePx, canvasPx, sheenPx, placPx: array[8, seq[byte]]
  titlePx: seq[byte]
  texWallL, texWallR, texFloor, texCeil, texEnd: seq[byte]

proc camXf(rot, ly, persp: float32): Xf =
  let a = rot * (PI / 180.0'f32)
  let b = ly * (PI / 180.0'f32)
  Xf(ca: cos(a), sa: sin(a), cb: cos(b), sb: sin(b),
     ox: cam.x, oy: cam.y, oz: cam.z, persp: persp)

proc viewAt(xf: Xf; wx, wy, wz: float32): (float32, float32, float32) {.inline.} =
  let yy = if xf.mir: 2.0'f32 * G_FLOORY - wy else: wy
  let oy = if xf.mir: 2.0'f32 * G_FLOORY - xf.oy else: xf.oy
  let dx = wx - xf.ox
  let dy = yy - oy
  let dz = wz - xf.oz
  let x1 = dx * xf.ca + dz * xf.sa
  let y1 = dy
  let z1 = -dx * xf.sa + dz * xf.ca
  (x1, y1 * xf.cb - z1 * xf.sb, y1 * xf.sb + z1 * xf.cb)

proc project(xf: Xf; wx, wy, wz: float32): (float32, float32, float32) {.inline.} =
  let v = xf.viewAt(wx, wy, wz)
  if v[2] >= -0.5'f32: return (0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, v[2])
  let f = -xf.persp * (float32(RW) / SW) / v[2]
  (float32(RW) * 0.5'f32 + v[0] * f, float32(RH) * 0.5'f32 + v[1] * f, v[2])

proc cssX(px: int32): float32 {.inline.} = (float32(px) + 0.5'f32) * SCALE
proc cssY(py: int32): float32 {.inline.} = (float32(py) + 0.5'f32) * SCALE

proc hit(xf: Xf; ndcX, ndcY: float32; allowFloor: bool): (int32, float32, float32, float32) =
  let dx1 = ndcX * xf.ca - xf.sa
  let dz1 = -ndcX * xf.sa - xf.ca
  let d = (dx1, ndcY * xf.cb - dz1 * xf.sb, ndcY * xf.sb + dz1 * xf.cb)
  let oy = if xf.mir: 2.0'f32 * G_FLOORY - xf.oy else: xf.oy
  var best = 1e30'f32
  var face = -1'i32
  var t: float32
  if d[0] < -1e-6'f32:
    t = (-G_HALF - xf.ox) / d[0]
    if t > 0.0'f32 and t < best: (face, best) = (F_WALLL, t)
  elif d[0] > 1e-6'f32:
    t = (G_HALF - xf.ox) / d[0]
    if t > 0.0'f32 and t < best: (face, best) = (F_WALLR, t)
  if d[1] > 1e-6'f32 and allowFloor:
    t = (G_FLOORY - oy) / d[1]
    if t > 0.0'f32 and t < best: (face, best) = (F_FLOOR, t)
  elif d[1] < -1e-6'f32 and allowFloor:
    t = (G_CEILY - oy) / d[1]
    if t > 0.0'f32 and t < best: (face, best) = (F_CEIL, t)
  if d[2] < -1e-6'f32:
    t = (G_ZFAR - xf.oz) / d[2]
    if t > 0.0'f32 and t < best: (face, best) = (F_END, t)
  if face < 0: return (-1'i32, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1e30'f32)
  let pz = xf.oz + d[2] * best
  let py = oy + d[1] * best
  let px = xf.ox + d[0] * best
  case face
  of F_WALLL: (face, G_ZNEAR - pz, py - G_CEILY, best)
  of F_WALLR: (face, pz - G_ZFAR, py - G_CEILY, best)
  of F_END:   (face, px + G_HALF, py - G_CEILY, best)
  of F_FLOOR: (face, px + G_HALF, pz - G_ZFAR, best)
  else:       (face, px + G_HALF, G_ZNEAR - pz, best)

proc sampleTex(tex: openArray[byte]; tw, th: int32; u, v: float32): uint32 =
  if tex.len < 4 or tw <= 0 or th <= 0: return 0'u32
  if u != u or v != v: return 0'u32
  let cu = cl(u, -1e6'f32, 1e6'f32)
  let cv = cl(v, -1e6'f32, 1e6'f32)
  let x0f = floor(cu)
  let y0f = floor(cv)
  let x0 = int32(x0f)
  let y0 = int32(y0f)
  let fx = cu - x0f
  let fy = cv - y0f
  var ax0 = x0 mod tw
  var ay0 = y0 mod th
  if ax0 < 0: ax0 += tw
  if ay0 < 0: ay0 += th
  let ax1 = if ax0 + 1 >= tw: 0'i32 else: ax0 + 1
  let ay1 = if ay0 + 1 >= th: 0'i32 else: ay0 + 1
  let i00 = (ay0 * tw + ax0) * 4
  let i10 = (ay0 * tw + ax1) * 4
  let i01 = (ay1 * tw + ax0) * 4
  let i11 = (ay1 * tw + ax1) * 4
  let w00 = (1.0'f32 - fx) * (1.0'f32 - fy)
  let w10 = fx * (1.0'f32 - fy)
  let w01 = (1.0'f32 - fx) * fy
  let w11 = fx * fy
  let a = w00 * float32(tex[i00 + 3]) + w10 * float32(tex[i10 + 3]) +
          w01 * float32(tex[i01 + 3]) + w11 * float32(tex[i11 + 3])
  let r = w00 * float32(tex[i00])     + w10 * float32(tex[i10]) +
          w01 * float32(tex[i01])     + w11 * float32(tex[i11])
  let g = w00 * float32(tex[i00 + 1]) + w10 * float32(tex[i10 + 1]) +
          w01 * float32(tex[i01 + 1]) + w11 * float32(tex[i11 + 1])
  let b = w00 * float32(tex[i00 + 2]) + w10 * float32(tex[i10 + 2]) +
          w01 * float32(tex[i01 + 2]) + w11 * float32(tex[i11 + 2])
  (uint32(clampByte(rnd32(a))) shl 24) or
  (uint32(clampByte(rnd32(r))) shl 16) or
  (uint32(clampByte(rnd32(g))) shl 8) or
  uint32(clampByte(rnd32(b)))

proc vlerp(a, b: array[6, float32]; t: float32): array[6, float32] {.inline.} =
  for k in 0 .. 5:
    result[k] = a[k] + (b[k] - a[k]) * t

proc scanRow(q: Quad; py: float32; xs: var array[4, float32];
             ed: var array[4, int32]; t01: var array[4, float32]): int =
  var n = 0
  for e in 0 ..< q.n:
    if n >= 4: break
    let a = q.v[e]
    let b = q.v[(e + 1) mod q.n]
    if (a[1] <= py and b[1] > py) or (b[1] <= py and a[1] > py):
      let t = (py - a[1]) / (b[1] - a[1])
      xs[n] = a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t
      t01[n] = t
      ed[n] = int32(e)
      inc n
  if n >= 2 and xs[1] < xs[0]:
    swap(xs[0], xs[1])
    swap(t01[0], t01[1])
    swap(ed[0], ed[1])
  n

proc quadPx(buf: var seq[byte]; rw, rh: int; q: Quad; tex: openArray[byte];
            tw, th: int32; alpha: float32; depth: openArray[float32]; dTest: bool) =
  var minY = 1e9'f32; var maxY = -1e9'f32
  var minX = 1e9'f32; var maxX = -1e9'f32
  for i in 0 ..< q.n:
    let c = q.v[i]
    if c[2] >= -0.5'f32: return
    if c[1] < minY: minY = c[1]
    if c[1] > maxY: maxY = c[1]
    if c[0] < minX: minX = c[0]
    if c[0] > maxX: maxX = c[0]
  let y0 = max(0'i32, int32(floor(minY)))
  let y1 = min(int32(rh) - 1, int32(ceil(maxY)))
  let x0 = max(0'i32, int32(floor(minX)))
  let x1 = min(int32(rw) - 1, int32(ceil(maxX)))
  if y1 < y0 or x1 < x0 or tex.len < 4: return

  var xs, t01, xs2, t12: array[4, float32]
  var ed, ed2: array[4, int32]
  for yy in y0 .. y1:
    let py = float32(yy) + 0.5'f32
    if scanRow(q, py, xs, ed, t01) >= 2:
      let ea = int(ed[0])
      let eb = int(ed[1])
      let a = vlerp(q.v[ea], q.v[(ea + 1) mod q.n], t01[0])
      let b = vlerp(q.v[eb], q.v[(eb + 1) mod q.n], t01[1])
      let lx = xs[0]
      let hx = xs[1]
      if hx >= lx:
        let iwA = a[5]
        let dw = b[5] - a[5]
        let duW = b[3] - a[3]
        let dvW = b[4] - a[4]
        let span = hx - lx
        var upx = 0.0'f32
        if span > 1e-4'f32:
          let iwMid = iwA + dw * 0.5'f32
          if abs(iwMid) > 1e-12'f32:
            upx = (duW * iwMid - (a[3] + duW * 0.5'f32) * dw) / (iwMid * iwMid * span)
        var vpy = 0.0'f32
        if scanRow(q, py + 1.0'f32, xs2, ed2, t12) >= 2 and xs2[1] > xs2[0]:
          let a2 = vlerp(q.v[int(ed2[0])], q.v[(int(ed2[0]) + 1) mod q.n], t12[0])
          let b2 = vlerp(q.v[int(ed2[1])], q.v[(int(ed2[1]) + 1) mod q.n], t12[1])
          let iw2 = a2[5] + (b2[5] - a2[5]) * 0.5'f32
          if abs(iw2) > 1e-12'f32 and abs(iwA + dw * 0.5'f32) > 1e-12'f32:
            vpy = (a2[4] + (b2[4] - a2[4]) * 0.5'f32) / iw2 -
                  (a[4] + dvW * 0.5'f32) / (iwA + dw * 0.5'f32)
        let nu = clamp(int32(ceil(abs(upx))), 1'i32, QPX_MAXSUB)
        let nv = clamp(int32(ceil(abs(vpy))), 1'i32, QPX_MAXSUB)
        let inv = 1.0'f32 / float32(nu * nv)
        let pxStart = max(x0, int32(floor(lx)))
        let pxEnd = min(x1, int32(ceil(hx)))
        for px in pxStart .. pxEnd:
          let pxf = float32(px) + 0.5'f32
          let t = if span > 1e-4'f32: (pxf - lx) / span else: 0.0'f32
          let iw = iwA + dw * t
          let z = if abs(iw) > 1e-12'f32: 1.0'f32 / iw else: a[2]
          if not dTest or -z <= depth[yy * int32(rw) + px] + 0.5'f32:
            if abs(iw) > 1e-12'f32:
              let u0 = (a[3] + duW * t) / iw
              let v0 = (a[4] + dvW * t) / iw
              var ar, ag, ab, aa: float32
              for j in 0 ..< nv:
                let vv = v0 + vpy * ((float32(j) + 0.5'f32) / float32(nv) - 0.5'f32)
                for i in 0 ..< nu:
                  let uu = u0 + upx * ((float32(i) + 0.5'f32) / float32(nu) - 0.5'f32)
                  let c = sampleTex(tex, tw, th, uu, vv)
                  let cr = rgbOf(c)
                  let ca = alphaOf(c)
                  ar += ca * float32(cr[0])
                  ag += ca * float32(cr[1])
                  ab += ca * float32(cr[2])
                  aa += ca
              ar *= inv; ag *= inv; ab *= inv; aa *= inv
              let a0 = aa * alpha
              if a0 > 0.0'f32:
                let sc = if aa > 1e-6'f32: 1.0'f32 / aa else: 0.0'f32
                let sr = (ar * sc) / 255.0'f32
                let sg = (ag * sc) / 255.0'f32
                let sb = (ab * sc) / 255.0'f32
                let idx = (yy * int32(rw) + px) * 4
                if a0 >= 1.0'f32:
                  buf[idx]     = byte(clampByte(rnd32(sr * 255.0'f32)))
                  buf[idx + 1] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(sg * 255.0'f32)))
                  buf[idx + 2] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(sb * 255.0'f32)))
                  buf[idx + 3] = 255'u8
                else:
                  let dr = float32(buf[idx]) / 255.0'f32
                  let dg = float32(buf[idx + 1]) / 255.0'f32
                  let db = float32(buf[idx + 2]) / 255.0'f32
                  let da = float32(buf[idx + 3]) / 255.0'f32
                  let na = a0 + da * (1.0'f32 - a0)
                  if na > 0.0'f32:
                    let nr = (sr * a0 + dr * da * (1.0'f32 - a0)) / na
                    let ng = (sg * a0 + dg * da * (1.0'f32 - a0)) / na
                    let nb = (sb * a0 + db * da * (1.0'f32 - a0)) / na
                    buf[idx]     = byte(clampByte(rnd32(nr * 255.0'f32)))
                    buf[idx + 1] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(ng * 255.0'f32)))
                    buf[idx + 2] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(nb * 255.0'f32)))
                    buf[idx + 3] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(na * 255.0'f32)))

proc drawSprite(xf: Xf; tex: openArray[byte]; texW, texH: int32; alpha: float32;
                corners: array[4, array[3, float32]]; uOff, uScale: float32;
                depth: openArray[float32]; buf: var seq[byte]) =
  var q: Quad
  let uvs = [
    (0.0'f32, 0.0'f32),
    (float32(texW), 0.0'f32),
    (float32(texW), float32(texH)),
    (0.0'f32, float32(texH))
  ]
  for i in 0 ..< 4:
    let p = xf.project(corners[i][0], corners[i][1], corners[i][2])
    if p[2] >= -0.5'f32: return
    let iw = 1.0'f32 / p[2]
    q.v[i] = [p[0], p[1], p[2], (uvs[i][0] * uScale + uOff) * iw, uvs[i][1] * iw, iw]
  q.n = 4
  var dTest = true
  let cX0 = corners[0][0]
  let cZ0 = corners[0][2]
  if abs(corners[0][0] - corners[2][0]) < 0.01'f32 and abs(cX0) > 1.0'f32:
    let wallX = if cX0 < 0.0'f32: -G_HALF else: G_HALF
    dTest = abs(cX0) > abs(wallX) + 0.5'f32
  elif abs(corners[0][2] - corners[2][2]) < 0.01'f32 and cZ0 < 0.0'f32:
    dTest = abs(cZ0) > abs(G_ZFAR) + 0.5'f32
  quadPx(buf, RW, RH, q, tex, texW, texH, alpha, depth, dTest)

proc beamAlphaAt(w: Work; py, pz: float32): float32 =
  let top = w.cy - w.h * 0.5'f32 - G_CEILY - 10.0'f32
  let bot = w.cy + w.h * 0.5'f32 - G_CEILY + 30.0'f32
  let lx = if w.side == 'L': pz - (w.z - BEAM_W * 0.5'f32)
           else: (w.z + BEAM_W * 0.5'f32) - pz
  let ly = py - G_CEILY
  if ly < 0.0'f32 or ly > BEAM_H or lx < 0.0'f32 or lx > BEAM_W: return 0.0'f32
  var inside = false
  var ax = 124.0'f32; var ay = 0.0'f32
  for k in 0 .. 3:
    let bx = if k == 0: 134.0'f32 elif k == 1: 0.0'f32 elif k == 2: 0.0'f32 else: 124.0'f32
    let by = if k == 0: 0.0'f32 elif k == 1: bot elif k == 2: top else: 0.0'f32
    if (ay > ly) != (by > ly):
      let xint = ax + (ly - ay) * (bx - ax) / (by - ay)
      if lx < xint: inside = not inside
    ax = bx; ay = by
  if not inside: return 0.0'f32
  let gx = sin(200.0'f32 * (PI / 180.0'f32))
  let gy = -cos(200.0'f32 * (PI / 180.0'f32))
  let half = (BEAM_W * 0.5'f32) * abs(gx) + (BEAM_H * 0.5'f32) * abs(gy)
  let t = cl(((lx - BEAM_W * 0.5'f32) * gx + (ly - BEAM_H * 0.5'f32) * gy) / half + 0.5'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  if t <= 0.55'f32: BEAM_A + (BEAM_B - BEAM_A) * (t / 0.55'f32)
  else: BEAM_B + (BEAM_C - BEAM_B) * ((t - 0.55'f32) / 0.45'f32)

proc drawBeams(xf: Xf; buf: var seq[byte]; bw, bh: int; cssPerPx: float32) =
  if mode != 0: return
  let bxL = -G_HALF + 75.0'f32
  let bxR = G_HALF - 75.0'f32
  const
    Z_LO = WORKS[7].z - BEAM_W * 0.5'f32
    Z_HI = WORKS[0].z + BEAM_W * 0.5'f32
  for py in 0 ..< bh:
    for qx in 0 ..< bw:
      var acc = 0.0'f32
      for sy in 0 .. 1:
        for sx in 0 .. 1:
          let cssXq = (float32(qx) + 0.5'f32 + (float32(sx) - 0.5'f32)) * cssPerPx
          let cssYq = (float32(py) + 0.5'f32 + (float32(sy) - 0.5'f32)) * cssPerPx
          let ndcX = (cssXq - SW * 0.5'f32) / PERSP
          let ndcY = (cssYq - SH * 0.5'f32) / PERSP
          let dx1 = ndcX * xf.ca - xf.sa
          let dz1 = -ndcX * xf.sa - xf.ca
          let d = (dx1, ndcY * xf.cb - dz1 * xf.sb, ndcY * xf.sb + dz1 * xf.cb)
          if d[0] < -1e-6'f32:
            let t = (bxL - xf.ox) / d[0]
            if t > 0.0'f32:
              let wz = xf.oz + d[2] * t
              let wy = xf.oy + d[1] * t
              if wz >= Z_LO and wz <= Z_HI:
                for i in 0 ..< 8:
                  if WORKS[i].side == 'L':
                    acc += beamAlphaAt(WORKS[i], wy, wz)
          elif d[0] > 1e-6'f32:
            let t = (bxR - xf.ox) / d[0]
            if t > 0.0'f32:
              let wz = xf.oz + d[2] * t
              let wy = xf.oy + d[1] * t
              if wz >= Z_LO and wz <= Z_HI:
                for i in 0 ..< 8:
                  if WORKS[i].side == 'R':
                    acc += beamAlphaAt(WORKS[i], wy, wz)
      if acc <= 0.0'f32: continue
      let a = acc * 0.25'f32
      let o = (py * bw + qx) * 4
      let dr = float32(buf[o]) / 255.0'f32
      let dg = float32(buf[o + 1]) / 255.0'f32
      let db = float32(buf[o + 2]) / 255.0'f32
      let da = float32(buf[o + 3]) / 255.0'f32
      let na = a + da * (1.0'f32 - a)
      if na > 0.0'f32:
        let k = (1.0'f32 - a) * da / na
        buf[o]     = byte(clampByte(rnd32(((BEAM_CR / 255.0'f32) * a + dr * k) * 255.0'f32)))
        buf[o + 1] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(((BEAM_CG / 255.0'f32) * a + dg * k) * 255.0'f32)))
        buf[o + 2] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(((BEAM_CB / 255.0'f32) * a + db * k) * 255.0'f32)))
        buf[o + 3] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(na * 255.0'f32)))

# ─────────────────────────── 11. Corridor Renderer & Atmosphere
const
  VEIL_GONE_S   = 0.315'f32
  VEIL_FADE_S   = 1.0'f32
  CANVAS_FADE_S = 1.1'f32
  T_READY_S: array[8, float32] = [
    0.030'f32, 0.046'f32, 0.062'f32, 0.078'f32,
    0.094'f32, 0.110'f32, 0.126'f32, 0.142'f32
  ]

proc canvasAlpha(i: int): float32 =
  cssEase(cl((appT - T_READY_S[i]) / CANVAS_FADE_S, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))

proc fogAmt(): float32 =
  let endDist = cam.z - G_ZFAR
  cl((endDist - 500.0'f32) / 1300.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32) *
    max(0.0'f32, 1.0'f32 - abs(cam.rot) / 55.0'f32)

proc put(buf: var seq[byte]; i: int32; c: uint32) {.inline.} =
  buf[i]     = byte((c shr 16) and 0xFF'u32)
  buf[i + 1] = byte((c shr 8) and 0xFF'u32)
  buf[i + 2] = byte(c and 0xFF'u32)
  buf[i + 3] = byte((c shr 24) and 0xFF'u32)

proc blit(buf: var seq[byte]; i: int32; c: uint32; a: float32) {.inline.} =
  if a <= 0.0'f32: return
  if a >= 1.0'f32:
    put(buf, i, c)
    return
  let dr = float32(buf[i]) / 255.0'f32
  let dg = float32(buf[i + 1]) / 255.0'f32
  let db = float32(buf[i + 2]) / 255.0'f32
  let da = float32(buf[i + 3]) / 255.0'f32
  let sr = float32((c shr 16) and 0xFF'u32) / 255.0'f32
  let sg = float32((c shr 8) and 0xFF'u32) / 255.0'f32
  let sb = float32(c and 0xFF'u32) / 255.0'f32
  let na = a + da * (1.0'f32 - a)
  if na <= 0.0'f32: return
  buf[i]     = byte(clampByte(rnd32((sr * a + dr * da * (1.0'f32 - a)) / na * 255.0'f32)))
  buf[i + 1] = byte(clampByte(rnd32((sg * a + dg * da * (1.0'f32 - a)) / na * 255.0'f32)))
  buf[i + 2] = byte(clampByte(rnd32((sb * a + db * da * (1.0'f32 - a)) / na * 255.0'f32)))
  buf[i + 3] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(na * 255.0'f32)))

proc bakeStageAndAtmosphere() =
  ## Precomputes the exact elliptical `.stage` background, `.fog` unit alpha
  ## field, and `.vignette` alpha field at RW x RH (640x400).
  stageBgBuf = newSeq[byte](RW * RH * 4)
  fogUnitA   = newSeq[float32](RW * RH)
  vigUnitA   = newSeq[float32](RW * RH)
  const
    stageStops: array[3, CStop] = [
      (0.00'f32, hex(0x1B1713)),
      (0.60'f32, hex(0x0C0A08)),
      (1.00'f32, hex(0x050404))
    ]
  let stageRX = SW * 0.90'f32
  let stageRY = SH * 0.70'f32
  let fogRX   = SW * 0.30'f32
  let fogRY   = SH * 0.34'f32
  let vigRX   = SW * 1.20'f32
  let vigRY   = SH * 0.95'f32
  for py in 0'i32 ..< int32(RH):
    let cy = cssY(py)
    let dyS = cy - SH * 0.45'f32
    let dyC = cy - SH * 0.50'f32
    for px in 0'i32 ..< int32(RW):
      let cx = cssX(px)
      let dxC = cx - SW * 0.50'f32
      let idx = py * int32(RW) + px
      let dStage = sqrt((dxC / stageRX) * (dxC / stageRX) + (dyS / stageRY) * (dyS / stageRY))
      put(stageBgBuf, idx * 4, csample(stageStops, dStage))
      let dFog = sqrt((dxC / fogRX) * (dxC / fogRX) + (dyC / fogRY) * (dyC / fogRY))
      fogUnitA[idx] =
        if dFog >= 1.0'f32: 0.0'f32
        elif dFog <= 0.45'f32: lerpF(0.62'f32, 0.34'f32, dFog / 0.45'f32)
        else: lerpF(0.34'f32, 0.0'f32, (dFog - 0.45'f32) / 0.55'f32)
      let dVig = sqrt((dxC / vigRX) * (dxC / vigRX) + (dyC / vigRY) * (dyC / vigRY))
      vigUnitA[idx] =
        if dVig <= 0.55'f32: 0.0'f32
        elif dVig >= 1.0'f32: 0.55'f32
        else: 0.55'f32 * ((dVig - 0.55'f32) / 0.45'f32)

proc renderCorridor() =
  let bobY = sin(bob) * 1.6'f32
  let sway = sin(idleT * 0.35'f32) * 0.5'f32
  let lx = if mode == 0: lookX * 7.0'f32 + sway else: lookX * 2.0'f32
  let ly = if mode == 0: lookY * 4.0'f32 + pitch else: lookY * 1.2'f32 + pitch
  let rot = cam.rot + lx
  var xr = camXf(rot, ly, PERSP)
  xr.oy = cam.y + bobY
  var xm = xr
  xm.mir = true

  # 1. Mirror group: reflected walls, end wall, and reflected paintings
  for py in 0'i32 ..< int32(RH):
    let ndcY = (cssY(py) - SH * 0.5'f32) / PERSP
    for px in 0'i32 ..< int32(RW):
      let ndcX = (cssX(px) - SW * 0.5'f32) / PERSP
      let h = xm.hit(ndcX, -ndcY, false)
      let o = (py * int32(RW) + px) * 4
      if h[0] >= 0:
        let c = case h[0]
          of F_WALLL: sampleTex(texWallL, TW_L, TH_L, h[1], h[2])
          of F_WALLR: sampleTex(texWallR, TW_L, TH_L, h[1], h[2])
          of F_END:   sampleTex(texEnd, TW_E, TH_E, h[1], h[2])
          else:       0'u32
        put(corrBuf, o, c)
        mbuf[py * int32(RW) + px] = h[3]
      else:
        corrBuf[o]     = stageBgBuf[o]
        corrBuf[o + 1] = stageBgBuf[o + 1]
        corrBuf[o + 2] = stageBgBuf[o + 2]
        corrBuf[o + 3] = 255'u8
        mbuf[py * int32(RW) + px] = 1e30'f32

  for i in 0 ..< 8:
    let w = WORKS[i]
    let ow = outerW(w)
    let oh = outerH(w)
    let x = paintX(w)
    let corners = [
      [x, w.cy - oh * 0.5'f32, w.z + ow * 0.5'f32],
      [x, w.cy - oh * 0.5'f32, w.z - ow * 0.5'f32],
      [x, w.cy + oh * 0.5'f32, w.z - ow * 0.5'f32],
      [x, w.cy + oh * 0.5'f32, w.z + ow * 0.5'f32]
    ]
    drawSprite(xm, spritePx[i], int32(ow * float32(SPRITE_SCALE)), int32(oh * float32(SPRITE_SCALE)),
               1.0'f32, corners, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, mbuf, corrBuf)
    drawSprite(xm, canvasPx[i], int32(ow * float32(SPRITE_SCALE)), int32(oh * float32(SPRITE_SCALE)),
               canvasAlpha(i), corners, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, mbuf, corrBuf)

  # 2. Real room: translucent marble floor over mirror, plus walls, end wall, ceiling
  for py in 0'i32 ..< int32(RH):
    let ndcY = (cssY(py) - SH * 0.5'f32) / PERSP
    for px in 0'i32 ..< int32(RW):
      let ndcX = (cssX(px) - SW * 0.5'f32) / PERSP
      let h = xr.hit(ndcX, ndcY, true)
      let idx = py * int32(RW) + px
      let o = idx * 4
      case h[0]
      of F_FLOOR:
        let c = sampleTex(texFloor, TW_F, TH_F, h[1], h[2])
        blit(corrBuf, o, c, alphaOf(c))
        dbuf[idx] = h[3]
      of F_WALLL:
        put(corrBuf, o, sampleTex(texWallL, TW_L, TH_L, h[1], h[2]))
        dbuf[idx] = h[3]
      of F_WALLR:
        put(corrBuf, o, sampleTex(texWallR, TW_L, TH_L, h[1], h[2]))
        dbuf[idx] = h[3]
      of F_END:
        put(corrBuf, o, sampleTex(texEnd, TW_E, TH_E, h[1], h[2]))
        dbuf[idx] = h[3]
      of F_CEIL:
        put(corrBuf, o, sampleTex(texCeil, TW_F, TH_F, h[1], h[2]))
        dbuf[idx] = h[3]
      else:
        corrBuf[o]     = stageBgBuf[o]
        corrBuf[o + 1] = stageBgBuf[o + 1]
        corrBuf[o + 2] = stageBgBuf[o + 2]
        corrBuf[o + 3] = 255'u8
        dbuf[idx] = 1e30'f32

  # 3. Real 3D sprites: end-wall title, framed works, sheens, placards, light beams
  drawSprite(xr, titlePx, TITLE_W * 4, TITLE_H * 4, 1.0'f32, [
    [-275.0'f32, -158.0'f32, G_ZFAR + 1.0'f32],
    [ 275.0'f32, -158.0'f32, G_ZFAR + 1.0'f32],
    [ 275.0'f32,   32.0'f32, G_ZFAR + 1.0'f32],
    [-275.0'f32,   32.0'f32, G_ZFAR + 1.0'f32]
  ], 0.0'f32, 0.25'f32, dbuf, corrBuf)

  for i in 0 ..< 8:
    let w = WORKS[i]
    let ow = outerW(w)
    let oh = outerH(w)
    let x = paintX(w)
    let corners = [
      [x, w.cy - oh * 0.5'f32, w.z + ow * 0.5'f32],
      [x, w.cy - oh * 0.5'f32, w.z - ow * 0.5'f32],
      [x, w.cy + oh * 0.5'f32, w.z - ow * 0.5'f32],
      [x, w.cy + oh * 0.5'f32, w.z + ow * 0.5'f32]
    ]
    drawSprite(xr, spritePx[i], int32(ow * float32(SPRITE_SCALE)), int32(oh * float32(SPRITE_SCALE)),
               1.0'f32, corners, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, dbuf, corrBuf)
    drawSprite(xr, canvasPx[i], int32(ow * float32(SPRITE_SCALE)), int32(oh * float32(SPRITE_SCALE)),
               canvasAlpha(i), corners, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, dbuf, corrBuf)
    if sheenPx[i].len > 0 and sheenPos > 0.0'f32:
      let inner = (ow - 2.0'f32 * w.fw) * float32(SPRITE_SCALE)
      let lo = -(sheenPos / 100.0'f32) * 2.2'f32 * inner
      let top = w.cy - oh * 0.5'f32 + w.fw
      let bot = w.cy + oh * 0.5'f32 - w.fw
      drawSprite(xr, sheenPx[i], sheenLayerW(int32(i)), sheenLayerH(int32(i)), 1.0'f32, [
        [x, top, w.z + ow * 0.5'f32],
        [x, top, w.z - ow * 0.5'f32],
        [x, bot, w.z - ow * 0.5'f32],
        [x, bot, w.z + ow * 0.5'f32]
      ], lo, 1.0'f32, dbuf, corrBuf)
    let pw = 66.0'f32
    let ph = 34.0'f32
    let pxw = placX(w)
    let pzw = placZ(w)
    let pyw = placY(w)
    drawSprite(xr, placPx[i], PLAC_W * 4, PLAC_H * 4, 1.0'f32, [
      [pxw, pyw - ph * 0.5'f32, pzw + pw * 0.5'f32],
      [pxw, pyw - ph * 0.5'f32, pzw - pw * 0.5'f32],
      [pxw, pyw + ph * 0.5'f32, pzw - pw * 0.5'f32],
      [pxw, pyw + ph * 0.5'f32, pzw + pw * 0.5'f32]
    ], 0.0'f32, 0.25'f32, dbuf, corrBuf)

  drawBeams(xr, corrBuf, RW, RH, SCALE)

  # 4. Dynamic camera-relative depth fog (.fog) & elliptical room vignette (.vignette)
  let fAmt = fogAmt()
  for idx in 0 ..< RW * RH:
    let af = fogUnitA[idx] * fAmt
    let av = vigUnitA[idx]
    if af > 0.0005'f32 or av > 0.0005'f32:
      let o = idx * 4
      var r = float32(corrBuf[o])
      var g = float32(corrBuf[o + 1])
      var b = float32(corrBuf[o + 2])
      if af > 0.0005'f32:
        let invF = 1.0'f32 - af
        r = r * invF + 8.0'f32 * af
        g = g * invF + 7.0'f32 * af
        b = b * invF + 6.0'f32 * af
      if av > 0.0005'f32:
        let invV = 1.0'f32 - av
        r *= invV; g *= invV; b *= invV
      corrBuf[o]     = byte(clampByte(rnd32(r)))
      corrBuf[o + 1] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(g)))
      corrBuf[o + 2] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(b)))
      corrBuf[o + 3] = 255'u8

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────── 12. HUD & Navigation
var
  heldLeft, heldFwd, heldBack, heldRight, heldApproach = false
  padSlot = -1
  padDownT = -10.0'f32

proc nearest(): int32 =
  let side = if cam.rot < -25.0'f32: 'L' elif cam.rot > 25.0'f32: 'R' else: '\0'
  var best = 0'i32
  var bd = 1e9'f32
  for i in 0 ..< 8:
    let w = WORKS[i]
    if side != '\0' and w.side != side: continue
    let ahead = cam.z - w.z
    var d = abs(ahead - (if side != '\0': 0.0'f32 else: 220.0'f32))
    if ahead < -60.0'f32 and side == '\0': d += 600.0'f32
    if d < bd:
      bd = d
      best = int32(i)
  best

proc stepBack(silent = false)

proc walkTo(z: float32) =
  if mode == 1: stepBack(true)
  tgt.z = max(Z_MIN, min(Z_MAX, z))
  tgt.x = 0.0'f32
  tgt.y = 0.0'f32
  if abs(tgt.rot) > 30.0'f32: tgt.rot = 0.0'f32

proc frameFor(i: int32) =
  let w = WORKS[i]
  let oh = outerH(w)
  let ow = outerW(w)
  let cardW = min(CARD_W, SW * 0.34'f32) + min(64.0'f32, max(20.0'f32, SW * 0.04'f32))
  let left = 48.0'f32
  let right = SW - cardW - 56.0'f32
  let aw = max(200.0'f32, right - left)
  let compW = ow + 26.0'f32 + 66.0'f32
  let compOff = (26.0'f32 + 66.0'f32) * 0.5'f32
  let s = min((SH * 0.7'f32) / oh, (aw * 0.94'f32) / compW)
  let dd = PERSP / s
  let cxs = (left + right) * 0.5'f32
  let shift = (SW * 0.5'f32 - cxs) / s + compOff
  let sign = if w.side == 'L': 1.0'f32 else: -1.0'f32
  tgt.x = if w.side == 'L': -G_HALF + dd else: G_HALF - dd
  tgt.y = w.cy
  tgt.z = w.z - sign * shift
  tgt.rot = if w.side == 'L': -90.0'f32 else: 90.0'f32

proc approach(i: int32) =
  if i < 0 or i > 7: return
  mode = 1
  current = i
  frameFor(i)
  cardT = 0.0'f32

proc stepBack(silent = false) =
  discard silent
  if mode != 1: return
  mode = 0
  let w = WORKS[current]
  tgt.x = 0.0'f32
  tgt.y = 0.0'f32
  tgt.rot = 0.0'f32
  tgt.z = max(Z_MIN, min(Z_MAX, w.z + 260.0'f32))
  cardT = -1.0'f32

proc stepWork(dir: int32) =
  let n = if current < 0: (if dir > 0: 0'i32 else: 7'i32)
          else: (current + dir + 8'i32) mod 8'i32
  approach(n)

proc drawBrand(d: var Draw) =
  baseText(d, BRAND_L, BRAND_T + 10.0'f32, 10.0'f32, C_KICK,
           "SALLE DES HUIT · OPEN AFTER DARK", 400)
  let titleBase = BRAND_T + 10.0'f32 + 8.0'f32 + 36.0'f32
  baseText(d, BRAND_L, titleBase, 40.96'f32, C_PAPER, "Galerie", 400)
  let galW = estTextW("Galerie ", 40.96'f32)
  baseText(d, BRAND_L + galW, titleBase, 40.96'f32, C_GOLDHI, "Nocturne", 400)
  rampH(d, BRAND_L, BRAND_T + 10.0'f32 + 8.0'f32 + 40.96'f32 + 14.0'f32, 56.0'f32, 1.0'f32, [
    (0.0'f32, C_GOLD),
    (1.0'f32, withA(C_GOLD, 0.0'f32))
  ], 24)

proc drawNear(d: var Draw) =
  if mode != 0: return
  let n = nearest()
  let w = WORKS[n]
  let rx = SW - NEAR_R
  rightText(d, rx, NEAR_T + 13.0'f32, 13.0'f32, C_GOLD,
            "No. " & ROMAN[n] & " · " & (if w.side == 'L': "left wall" else: "right wall"), 400)
  rightText(d, rx, NEAR_T + 13.0'f32 + 4.0'f32 + 22.0'f32 * 1.15'f32, 22.0'f32, C_PAPER,
            w.title, 400)
  rightText(d, rx, NEAR_T + 13.0'f32 + 4.0'f32 + 22.0'f32 * 1.15'f32 + 5.0'f32 + 11.0'f32,
            11.0'f32, C_MUTED, w.artist & ", " & w.year, 400)
  let bw = 122.0'f32
  let bh = 26.0'f32
  let bx = rx - bw
  let by = NEAR_T + 13.0'f32 + 4.0'f32 + 22.0'f32 * 1.15'f32 + 5.0'f32 + 11.0'f32 + 12.0'f32
  d = d.rect(bx, by, bw, bh, rgba(20, 17, 14, 0.6'f32), bh * 0.5'f32)
  d = rectStroke(d, bx, by, bw, bh, bh * 0.5'f32, rgba(0xC9, 0xA4, 0x5C, 0.45'f32), 1.0'f32)
  centreText(d, bx + bw * 0.5'f32, by + 17.0'f32, 10.0'f32, C_GOLDHI, "APPROACH ↵")

proc pbtn(d: var Draw; cx, cy, r: float32; held: bool) =
  let bg = if held: rgba(201, 164, 92, 0.22'f32) else: rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.04'f32)
  let bd = if held: C_GOLD else: rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.18'f32)
  d = d.circle(cx, cy, r, bg)
  d = strokeCircle(d, cx, cy, r - 0.5'f32, bd, 1.0'f32)

proc drawPad(d: var Draw) =
  if mode != 0: return
  let gap = 10.0'f32
  let wideW = 108.0'f32
  let inner = 4.0'f32 * 44.0'f32 + wideW + gap * 5.0'f32 + 6.0'f32
  let padW = inner + 16.0'f32
  let padH = 60.0'f32
  let px0 = (SW - padW) * 0.5'f32
  let py0 = SH - PAD_B - padH
  d = d.rect(px0, py0, padW, padH, rgba(16, 14, 12, 0.55'f32), padH * 0.5'f32)
  d = rectStroke(d, px0, py0, padW, padH, padH * 0.5'f32, rgba(201, 164, 92, 0.22'f32), 1.0'f32)
  var bx = px0 + 8.0'f32 + 22.0'f32
  let by = py0 + 8.0'f32 + 22.0'f32

  # Left turn button
  pbtn(d, bx, by, 22.0'f32, heldLeft)
  d = d.moveTo(bx - 2.0'f32, by - 5.0'f32).lineTo(bx - 7.0'f32, by).lineTo(bx - 2.0'f32, by + 5.0'f32)
       .moveTo(bx - 6.5'f32, by).lineTo(bx + 3.0'f32, by)
       .quadTo(bx + 7.0'f32, by, bx + 7.0'f32, by + 4.0'f32).lineTo(bx + 7.0'f32, by + 5.0'f32)
       .stroke(C_PAPER, 1.5'f32)

  # Walk forward button
  bx += 44.0'f32 + gap
  pbtn(d, bx, by, 22.0'f32, heldFwd)
  d = d.moveTo(bx, by + 6.0'f32).lineTo(bx, by - 6.0'f32)
       .moveTo(bx - 5.0'f32, by - 1.0'f32).lineTo(bx, by - 6.0'f32).lineTo(bx + 5.0'f32, by - 1.0'f32)
       .stroke(C_PAPER, 1.5'f32)

  # Walk backward button
  bx += 44.0'f32 + gap
  pbtn(d, bx, by, 22.0'f32, heldBack)
  d = d.moveTo(bx, by - 6.0'f32).lineTo(bx, by + 6.0'f32)
       .moveTo(bx - 5.0'f32, by + 1.0'f32).lineTo(bx, by + 6.0'f32).lineTo(bx + 5.0'f32, by + 1.0'f32)
       .stroke(C_PAPER, 1.5'f32)

  # Right turn button
  bx += 44.0'f32 + gap
  pbtn(d, bx, by, 22.0'f32, heldRight)
  d = d.moveTo(bx + 2.0'f32, by - 5.0'f32).lineTo(bx + 7.0'f32, by).lineTo(bx + 2.0'f32, by + 5.0'f32)
       .moveTo(bx + 6.5'f32, by).lineTo(bx - 3.0'f32, by)
       .quadTo(bx - 7.0'f32, by, bx - 7.0'f32, by + 4.0'f32).lineTo(bx - 7.0'f32, by + 5.0'f32)
       .stroke(C_PAPER, 1.5'f32)

  # Separator + View pill
  bx += 22.0'f32 + gap
  d = d.rect(bx + 1.0'f32, by - 12.0'f32, 1.0'f32, 24.0'f32, rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.14'f32))
  bx += 6.0'f32 + gap
  let pillBg = if heldApproach: rgba(201, 164, 92, 0.22'f32) else: rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.04'f32)
  let pillBd = if heldApproach: C_GOLD else: rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.18'f32)
  d = d.rect(bx, by - 22.0'f32, wideW, 44.0'f32, pillBg, 22.0'f32)
  d = rectStroke(d, bx, by - 22.0'f32, wideW, 44.0'f32, 22.0'f32, pillBd, 1.0'f32)
  let ex = bx + 26.0'f32
  d = d.moveTo(ex - 8.0'f32, by)
       .curveTo(ex - 4.0'f32, by - 6.0'f32, ex + 4.0'f32, by - 6.0'f32, ex + 8.0'f32, by)
       .curveTo(ex + 4.0'f32, by + 6.0'f32, ex - 4.0'f32, by + 6.0'f32, ex - 8.0'f32, by)
       .close().stroke(C_GOLDHI, 1.4'f32)
  d = d.circle(ex, by, 2.4'f32, C_GOLDHI)
  centreText(d, bx + 68.0'f32, by + 3.5'f32, 10.0'f32, C_GOLDHI, "VIEW")

proc drawPlan(d: var Draw) =
  if mode != 0: return
  let boxW = 268.0'f32
  let boxH = 12.0'f32 + 11.0'f32 + PLAN_SVG_H + 9.0'f32 + 26.0'f32 + 10.0'f32
  let bx0 = PLAN_L
  let by0 = SH - PLAN_B - boxH
  d = d.rect(bx0, by0, boxW, boxH, rgba(16, 14, 12, 0.62'f32), 10.0'f32)
  d = rectStroke(d, bx0, by0, boxW, boxH, 10.0'f32, rgba(201, 164, 92, 0.2'f32), 1.0'f32)
  baseText(d, bx0 + 14.0'f32, by0 + 20.0'f32, 9.0'f32, C_MUTED, "FLOOR PLAN", 400)
  let pz = G_ZNEAR - cam.z
  let tenths = rnd32(pz / 100.0'f32) * 3
  let mstr = $(tenths div 10) & (if tenths mod 10 == 0: "" else: "." & $(tenths mod 10)) & " m"
  let pos = if pz < 380.0'f32: "Entrance"
            elif pz > 2350.0'f32: "End wall"
            else: mstr
  rightText(d, bx0 + boxW - 14.0'f32, by0 + 20.0'f32, 9.0'f32, C_GOLD, pos, 400)

  let s = PLAN_W / 2820.0'f32
  let ox = bx0 + 14.0'f32 + 60.0'f32 * s
  let oy = by0 + 23.0'f32 + 90.0'f32 * s
  proc mpx(u: float32): float32 = ox + u * s
  proc mpy(v: float32): float32 = oy + v * s

  d = d.rect(mpx(0), mpy(0), 2700.0'f32 * s, 600.0'f32 * s, rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.05'f32))
  d = rectStroke(d, mpx(0), mpy(0), 2700.0'f32 * s, 600.0'f32 * s, 0.0'f32,
                 rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.35'f32), 1.0'f32)
  d = d.moveTo(mpx(2700.0'f32), mpy(0)).lineTo(mpx(2700.0'f32), mpy(600.0'f32)).stroke(C_GOLD, 2.0'f32)
  var dg = 150.0'f32
  while dg < 450.0'f32:
    d = d.moveTo(mpx(0), mpy(dg)).lineTo(mpx(0), mpy(min(450.0'f32, dg + 35.0'f32)))
         .stroke(rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.35'f32), 1.0'f32)
    dg += 65.0'f32

  for i in 0 ..< 8:
    let w = WORKS[i]
    let mx = G_ZNEAR - w.z - w.w * 0.5'f32
    let my = if w.side == 'L': -38.0'f32 else: 608.0'f32
    d = d.rect(mpx(mx), mpy(my), w.w * s, 30.0'f32 * s, MARK_COL[i], 6.0'f32 * s)

  let cx = mpx(G_ZNEAR - cam.z)
  let cy = mpy(cam.x + G_HALF)
  d = d.save().translate(cx, cy).rotate(cam.rot)
  d = d.moveTo(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32).lineTo(420.0'f32 * s, -240.0'f32 * s)
  d = traceArcPoints(d, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 480.0'f32 * s, -29.74'f32, 59.48'f32, false)
  d = d.close().fill(rgba(236, 212, 154, 0.28'f32))
  d = d.restore()
  d = d.circle(cx, cy, 46.0'f32 * s, C_GOLDHI)
  d = strokeCircle(d, cx, cy, 46.0'f32 * s - 0.75'f32, hex(0x1A1511), 1.5'f32)

  var ix = bx0 + 14.0'f32
  let iy = by0 + boxH - 10.0'f32 - 26.0'f32
  let bw = (boxW - 28.0'f32 - 7.0'f32 * 4.0'f32) / 8.0'f32
  for i in 0 ..< 8:
    if mode == 1 and i == current:
      d = d.rect(ix, iy, bw, 26.0'f32, C_GOLD, 4.0'f32)
      centreText(d, ix + bw * 0.5'f32, iy + 18.0'f32, 11.0'f32, hex(0x15110C), ROMAN[i])
    else:
      d = rectStroke(d, ix, iy, bw, 26.0'f32, 4.0'f32, rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.12'f32), 1.0'f32)
      centreText(d, ix + bw * 0.5'f32, iy + 18.0'f32, 11.0'f32, C_MUTED, ROMAN[i])
    ix += bw + 4.0'f32

proc drawHint(d: var Draw) =
  if mode != 0: return
  const
    line0 = "W S walk · A D look · Enter view · Esc step back"
    line1 = "click a painting, or the floor to walk there"
  let bw = 264.0'f32
  let bh = 54.0'f32
  let bx = SW - HINT_R - bw
  let by = SH - HINT_B - bh
  d = d.rect(bx, by, bw, bh, rgba(16, 14, 12, 0.62'f32), 10.0'f32)
  d = rectStroke(d, bx, by, bw, bh, 10.0'f32, rgba(201, 164, 92, 0.2'f32), 1.0'f32)
  rightText(d, bx + bw - 14.0'f32, by + 23.0'f32, 10.5'f32, hex(0xCBBD9F), line0, 400)
  rightText(d, bx + bw - 14.0'f32, by + 41.0'f32, 10.5'f32, hex(0xCBBD9F), line1, 400)

proc drawCard(d: var Draw) =
  if cardT < 0.0'f32 or current < 0 or current > 7: return
  let w = WORKS[current]
  let t = cl(cardT, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  let ease = cubicBezier(0.2'f32, 0.8'f32, 0.2'f32, 1.0'f32, t)
  let slide = (1.0'f32 - ease) * 24.0'f32
  let op = if t >= 0.833'f32: 1.0'f32 else: cl(t / 0.833'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  let cw = CARD_W
  let x0 = SW - CARD_R - cw + slide
  let ch = 470.0'f32
  let y0 = SH * 0.5'f32 - ch * 0.5'f32

  d = d.save().alpha(op)
  rampV(d, x0, y0, cw, ch, [
    (0.0'f32, rgba(22, 19, 16, 0.92'f32)),
    (1.0'f32, rgba(14, 12, 10, 0.94'f32))
  ], 24)
  d = rectStroke(d, x0, y0, cw, ch, 4.0'f32, rgba(201, 164, 92, 0.28'f32), 1.0'f32)

  var y = y0 + 30.0'f32
  baseText(d, x0 + 30.0'f32, y + 13.0'f32, 13.0'f32, C_GOLD, "No. " & ROMAN[current] & " / VIII", 400)
  y += 23.0'f32
  baseText(d, x0 + 30.0'f32, y + 31.0'f32, 31.0'f32, C_PAPER, w.title, 400)
  y += 31.0'f32 * 1.08'f32 + 10.0'f32
  baseText(d, x0 + 30.0'f32, y + 13.0'f32, 13.0'f32, C_PAPER, w.artist, 400)
  let artW = estTextW(w.artist, 13.0'f32)
  baseText(d, x0 + 30.0'f32 + artW, y + 13.0'f32, 13.0'f32,
           C_MUTED, " (" & w.life & ", fictional)", 400)

  y += 36.0'f32
  d = d.rect(x0 + 30.0'f32, y, cw - 60.0'f32, 1.0'f32, rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.12'f32))
  var ry = y + 25.0'f32
  const FLD = ["YEAR", "MEDIUM", "SIZE"]
  let VAL = [w.year, w.medium, w.dims]
  for i in 0 .. 2:
    baseText(d, x0 + 30.0'f32, ry + 9.5'f32, 9.5'f32, C_MUTED, FLD[i], 400)
    baseText(d, x0 + 96.0'f32, ry + 12.0'f32, 12.0'f32, C_PAPER, VAL[i], 400)
    if i < 2: ry += 19.0'f32
  ry += 18.0'f32
  y = ry
  d = d.rect(x0 + 30.0'f32, y - 16.0'f32, cw - 60.0'f32, 1.0'f32, rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.12'f32))

  var line = ""
  for word in w.note.split(' '):
    if line.len > 0 and (line & " " & word).len > 46:
      baseText(d, x0 + 30.0'f32, y + 15.0'f32, 15.0'f32, hex(0xD9CFBD), line, 400)
      y += 15.0'f32 * 1.55'f32
      line = word
    else:
      line = if line.len == 0: word else: line & " " & word
  if line.len > 0:
    baseText(d, x0 + 30.0'f32, y + 15.0'f32, 15.0'f32, hex(0xD9CFBD), line, 400)
    y += 15.0'f32 * 1.55'f32

  y += 14.0'f32
  baseText(d, x0 + 30.0'f32, y + 10.0'f32, 10.0'f32, hex(0x7D705C),
           "Generated live in your browser · seed " & $w.seed, 400)

  let navY = y0 + ch - 24.0'f32
  var nx = x0 + 30.0'f32
  const NAVL = ["← PREV", "STEP BACK", "NEXT →"]
  for i in 0 .. 2:
    let bw = if i == 1: 128.0'f32 else: 96.0'f32
    let bc = if i == 1: rgba(201, 164, 92, 0.45'f32) else: rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.18'f32)
    let tc = if i == 1: C_GOLDHI else: C_PAPER
    d = d.rect(nx, navY - 38.0'f32, bw, 38.0'f32, rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.03'f32), 3.0'f32)
    d = rectStroke(d, nx, navY - 38.0'f32, bw, 38.0'f32, 3.0'f32, bc, 1.0'f32)
    centreText(d, nx + bw * 0.5'f32, navY - 15.0'f32, 10.0'f32, tc, NAVL[i])
    nx += bw + 8.0'f32
  d = d.restore()

proc drawVeil(d: var Draw) =
  let v = cl(veilT, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  if v <= 0.001'f32: return
  d = d.save().alpha(v)
  d = d.rect(0, 0, SW, SH, C_NIGHT)
  centreText(d, SW * 0.5'f32, SH * 0.5'f32 - 10.0'f32, 26.0'f32, C_PAPER, "Galerie Nocturne", 400)
  trackedCentreText(d, SW * 0.5'f32, SH * 0.5'f32 + 16.0'f32, 10.0'f32, 0.34'f32, C_MUTED,
                    "HANGING EIGHT WORKS", 400)
  d = d.rect(SW * 0.5'f32 - 90.0'f32, SH * 0.5'f32 + 36.0'f32, 180.0'f32, 1.0'f32,
             rgba(241, 233, 218, 0.12'f32))
  let barProg = cl(appT / VEIL_GONE_S, 0.375'f32, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.rect(SW * 0.5'f32 - 90.0'f32, SH * 0.5'f32 + 36.0'f32, 180.0'f32 * barProg, 1.0'f32, C_GOLD)
  d = d.restore()

# ──────────────────────────── 13. Hit-Testing, Bake & Main Event Loop
proc onNearApproach(cx, cy: float32): bool =
  if mode != 0: return false
  let rx = SW - NEAR_R
  let bw = 122.0'f32
  let bh = 26.0'f32
  let bx = rx - bw
  let by = NEAR_T + 13.0'f32 + 4.0'f32 + 22.0'f32 * 1.15'f32 + 5.0'f32 + 11.0'f32 + 12.0'f32
  cx >= bx and cx <= bx + bw and cy >= by and cy <= by + bh

proc onPad(cx, cy: float32): int32 =
  let gap = 10.0'f32
  let wideW = 108.0'f32
  let padW = 4.0'f32 * 44.0'f32 + wideW + gap * 5.0'f32 + 6.0'f32 + 16.0'f32
  let padH = 60.0'f32
  let px0 = (SW - padW) * 0.5'f32
  let py0 = SH - PAD_B - padH
  if cx < px0 or cx > px0 + padW or cy < py0 or cy > py0 + padH: return -1
  var bx = px0 + 8.0'f32 + 22.0'f32
  let by = py0 + 8.0'f32 + 22.0'f32
  for i in 0 .. 3:
    let dx = cx - bx
    let dy = cy - by
    if dx * dx + dy * dy <= 24.0'f32 * 24.0'f32: return int32(i)
    bx += 44.0'f32 + gap
  if cx >= bx - 10.0'f32: return 4'i32
  -1'i32

proc onIdx(cx, cy: float32): int32 =
  let boxW = 268.0'f32
  let boxH = 12.0'f32 + 11.0'f32 + PLAN_SVG_H + 9.0'f32 + 26.0'f32 + 10.0'f32
  let bx0 = PLAN_L
  let by0 = SH - PLAN_B - boxH
  if cx < bx0 or cx > bx0 + boxW or cy < by0 or cy > by0 + boxH: return -1
  let s = PLAN_W / 2820.0'f32
  let ox = bx0 + 14.0'f32 + 60.0'f32 * s
  let oy = by0 + 23.0'f32 + 90.0'f32 * s
  for i in 0 ..< 8:
    let w = WORKS[i]
    let mx = ox + (G_ZNEAR - w.z - w.w * 0.5'f32) * s
    let mw = w.w * s
    let my = oy + (if w.side == 'L': -38.0'f32 else: 608.0'f32) * s
    let mh = 30.0'f32 * s
    if cx >= mx and cx <= mx + mw and cy >= my and cy <= my + mh:
      return 20'i32 + int32(i)
  let iy = by0 + boxH - 10.0'f32 - 26.0'f32
  if cy < iy or cy > iy + 26.0'f32: return -1
  let bw = (boxW - 28.0'f32 - 7.0'f32 * 4.0'f32) / 8.0'f32
  var ix = bx0 + 14.0'f32
  for i in 0 .. 7:
    if cx >= ix and cx <= ix + bw: return int32(i)
    ix += bw + 4.0'f32
  -1'i32

proc onCardNav(cx, cy: float32): int32 =
  if cardT < 0.5'f32 or current < 0: return -1
  let x0 = SW - CARD_R - CARD_W
  let y0 = SH * 0.5'f32 - 235.0'f32 + 470.0'f32 - 62.0'f32
  if cx < x0 or cx > x0 + CARD_W or cy < y0 or cy > y0 + 38.0'f32: return -1
  var nx = x0 + 30.0'f32
  for i in 0 .. 2:
    let bw = if i == 1: 128.0'f32 else: 96.0'f32
    if cx >= nx and cx <= nx + bw: return int32(i)
    nx += bw + 8.0'f32
  -1'i32

proc clickAt(cx, cy: float32) =
  let n = onIdx(cx, cy)
  if n >= 20: approach(n - 20); return
  if n >= 0:  approach(n); return
  if mode == 1:
    let c = onCardNav(cx, cy)
    if c >= 0:
      case c
      of 0: stepWork(-1)
      of 1: stepBack()
      else: stepWork(1)
      return
  else:
    if onNearApproach(cx, cy):
      approach(nearest())
      return
    let p = onPad(cx, cy)
    if p >= 0:
      if appT - padDownT < 0.25'f32:
        case p
        of 0: tgt.rot = max(-90.0'f32, tgt.rot - 45.0'f32)
        of 1: tgt.z   = max(Z_MIN, tgt.z - 240.0'f32)
        of 2: tgt.z   = min(Z_MAX, tgt.z + 240.0'f32)
        of 3: tgt.rot = min(90.0'f32, tgt.rot + 45.0'f32)
        else: approach(nearest())
      return

  if not sceneReady: return
  let lx = if mode == 0: lookX * 7.0'f32 + sin(idleT * 0.35'f32) * 0.5'f32 else: lookX * 2.0'f32
  let ly = if mode == 0: lookY * 4.0'f32 + pitch else: lookY * 1.2'f32 + pitch
  var xf = camXf(cam.rot + lx, ly, PERSP)
  xf.oy = cam.y + sin(bob) * 1.6'f32
  let ndcX = (cx - SW * 0.5'f32) / PERSP
  let ndcY = (cy - SH * 0.5'f32) / PERSP
  let h = xf.hit(ndcX, ndcY, true)
  case h[0]
  of F_WALLL, F_WALLR:
    let side = if h[0] == F_WALLL: 'L' else: 'R'
    let z = if side == 'L': G_ZNEAR - h[1] else: h[1] + G_ZFAR
    var best = -1'i32
    var bd = 1e9'f32
    for i in 0 ..< 8:
      let w = WORKS[i]
      if w.side != side: continue
      let dd = abs(w.z - z)
      if dd < bd:
        bd = dd
        best = int32(i)
    if best >= 0 and bd < 320.0'f32: approach(best) else: walkTo(z + 200.0'f32)
  of F_FLOOR:
    walkTo(h[2] + G_ZFAR + 120.0'f32)
  of F_END:
    walkTo(G_ZFAR + 260.0'f32)
  else:
    if mode == 1: stepBack()

proc bakeArt(i: int32) =
  let w = WORKS[i]
  let pw = int32(w.w * float32(RES))
  let ph = int32(w.h * float32(RES))
  var r = rngInit(w.seed)
  let n = makeNoise(w.seed * 7 + 3)
  var d = Draw()
  case w.gen
  of gSunset: genSunset(d, float32(pw), float32(ph), r, n)
  of gGrid:   genGrid(d, float32(pw), float32(ph), r)
  of gField:  genColorField(d, float32(pw), float32(ph), r, n)
  of gOp:     genOp(pw, ph, r, artLayer[i])
  of gInk:    genInk(d, float32(pw), float32(ph), r, n)
  of gGeo:    genGeometric(d, float32(pw), float32(ph), r)
  of gFlow:   genFlow(d, float32(pw), float32(ph), r, n)
  of gMosaic: genMosaic(d, float32(pw), float32(ph), r)
  if w.gen != gOp:
    artLayer[i].draw(d)
  if w.gen == gMosaic:
    radialOverlayLayer(artLayer[i], int(pw), int(ph),
                       float32(pw) * 0.30'f32, float32(ph) * 0.25'f32, float32(pw) * 0.80'f32,
                       rgba(255, 240, 200, 0.14'f32), rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18'f32))
  case w.gen
  of gSunset: discard
  of gGrid:   grainPass(artLayer[i], int(pw), int(ph), 10.0'f32, r)
  of gField:  grainPass(artLayer[i], int(pw), int(ph), 12.0'f32, r)
  of gOp:     grainPass(artLayer[i], int(pw), int(ph), 7.0'f32, r)
  of gInk:    grainPass(artLayer[i], int(pw), int(ph), 8.0'f32, r)
  of gGeo:    grainPass(artLayer[i], int(pw), int(ph), 14.0'f32, r)
  of gFlow:   grainPass(artLayer[i], int(pw), int(ph), 8.0'f32, r)
  of gMosaic: discard

proc bakeAll() =
  ## Runs all artwork, frame, placard, title, and room texture bakes upfront
  ## so `corrLayer` and `corrBuf` are always valid and ready from Frame 0.
  for i in 0'i32 .. 7'i32:
    artLayer[i] = newLayer(int(WORKS[i].w * float32(RES)), int(WORKS[i].h * float32(RES)))
    bakeArt(i)
    buildSprite(i)
    buildPlacard(i)
    if WORKS[i].gold or WORKS[i].key == "mosaic":
      buildSheen(i)
  buildTitle()
  let plas = plasterTex()
  texWallL = wallTex('L', plas)
  texWallR = wallTex('R', plas)
  texFloor = floorTex(marbleTex(makeNoise(77)))
  texCeil  = ceilTex()
  texEnd   = endTex()
  bakeStageAndAtmosphere()

  corrLayer = newLayer(RW, RH)
  corrBuf   = newSeq[byte](RW * RH * 4)
  dbuf      = newSeq[float32](RW * RH)
  mbuf      = newSeq[float32](RW * RH)
  for i in 0 ..< 8:
    let w = WORKS[i]
    let sw = int(outerW(w) * float32(SPRITE_SCALE))
    let sh = int(outerH(w) * float32(SPRITE_SCALE))
    spritePx[i] = spriteLayer[i].read(0, 0, sw, sh)
    canvasPx[i] = canvasLayer[i].read(0, 0, sw, sh)
    if w.gold or w.key == "mosaic":
      sheenPx[i] = sheenLayer[i].read(0, 0, int(sheenLayerW(int32(i))), int(sheenLayerH(int32(i))))
    placPx[i] = placLayer[i].read(0, 0, PLAC_W * 4, PLAC_H * 4)
  titlePx = titleLayer.read(0, 0, TITLE_W * 4, TITLE_H * 4)
  sceneReady = true

proc tick() =
  frameDtPub = frameDt()
  appT += frameDtPub
  let dt = min(0.05'f32, frameDtPub)

  let fwd = if keyHeld('w') or keyHeld(keyUp): 1'i32
            elif keyHeld('s') or keyHeld(keyDown): -1'i32 else: 0'i32
  let strafe = if keyHeld('d'): 1'i32
               elif keyHeld('a'): -1'i32 else: 0'i32
  let turn = if keyHeld(keyRight): 1'i32
             elif keyHeld(keyLeft): -1'i32 else: 0'i32

  heldFwd      = fwd > 0 or padSlot == 1
  heldBack     = fwd < 0 or padSlot == 2
  heldRight    = turn > 0 or strafe > 0 or padSlot == 3
  heldLeft     = turn < 0 or strafe < 0 or padSlot == 0
  heldApproach = false

  if mode == 0:
    if fwd != 0:
      tgt.z = max(Z_MIN, min(Z_MAX, tgt.z - float32(fwd) * 520.0'f32 * dt))
    if strafe != 0:
      tgt.x = max(-STRAFE_X, min(STRAFE_X, tgt.x + float32(strafe) * 420.0'f32 * dt))
    if turn != 0:
      tgt.rot = max(-90.0'f32, min(90.0'f32, tgt.rot + float32(turn) * 110.0'f32 * dt))
    case padSlot
    of 0: tgt.rot = max(-90.0'f32, tgt.rot - 110.0'f32 * dt)
    of 1: tgt.z   = max(Z_MIN, tgt.z - 520.0'f32 * dt)
    of 2: tgt.z   = min(Z_MAX, tgt.z + 520.0'f32 * dt)
    of 3: tgt.rot = min(90.0'f32, tgt.rot + 110.0'f32 * dt)
    else: discard

  let k = 1.0'f32 - exp(-dt * 3.4'f32)
  let vmax = 1300.0'f32 * dt
  let rmax = 150.0'f32 * dt
  proc stepT(a, b, lim: float32): float32 =
    var s = (b - a) * k
    if s > lim: s = lim
    if s < -lim: s = -lim
    a + s

  let oz = cam.z
  cam.x   = stepT(cam.x, tgt.x, vmax)
  cam.y   = stepT(cam.y, tgt.y, vmax)
  cam.z   = stepT(cam.z, tgt.z, vmax)
  cam.rot = stepT(cam.rot, tgt.rot, rmax)
  bob += abs(cam.z - oz) * 0.045'f32
  idleT += dt

  let lk = 1.0'f32 - exp(-dt * 4.0'f32)
  lookX += (lookTX - lookX) * lk
  lookY += (lookTY - lookY) * lk
  pitch += (tgtPitch - pitch) * lk

  var sh = 50.0'f32 + (cam.z * 0.08'f32 + cam.x * 0.1'f32 + cam.rot * 0.4'f32)
  sh = fmod32(sh, 60.0'f32)
  if sh < 0.0'f32: sh += 60.0'f32
  sheenPos = 50.0'f32 + sh

  veilT = 1.0'f32 - cssEase(cl((appT - VEIL_GONE_S) / VEIL_FADE_S, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))
  if mode == 1:
    let settled = abs(cam.x - tgt.x) < 6.0'f32 and abs(cam.z - tgt.z) < 6.0'f32 and
                  abs(cam.rot - tgt.rot) < 2.0'f32
    if settled and cardT >= 0.0'f32:
      cardT = min(1.0'f32, cardT + dt / 0.6'f32)

var
  page: Node
  scene: Node
  ready = false

proc buildScene(): Draw =
  result = Draw()
  if not ready or not sceneReady: return
  renderCorridor()
  corrLayer.write(0, 0, RW, RH, corrBuf)
  result = result.layer(corrLayer, 0, 0, SW, SH)
  drawBrand(result)
  drawNear(result)
  drawPad(result)
  drawPlan(result)
  drawHint(result)
  drawCard(result)
  drawVeil(result)

proc repaint() =
  if not ready: return
  scene.set(pDraw, buildScene().cmds)

proc main() =
  page = window(title = "Galerie Nocturne", w = int32(SW), h = int32(SH))
  scene = canvas(page, int32(SW), int32(SH))
  bakeAll()

  scene.addHandler(evPointerDown, proc() =
    let (px, py) = pointerPos()
    let hudHit =
      if mode == 1: onIdx(px, py) >= 0 or onCardNav(px, py) >= 0
      else: onIdx(px, py) >= 0 or onPad(px, py) >= 0 or onNearApproach(px, py)
    dragOn = not hudHit
    dragMoved = false
    dragX = px; dragY = py
    dragRot = tgt.rot; dragPitch = tgtPitch
    if mode == 0:
      padSlot = onPad(px, py)
      if padSlot >= 0: padDownT = appT
    repaint()
  )

  scene.addHandler(evPointerMove, proc() =
    let (px, py) = pointerPos()
    lookTX = (px / SW - 0.5'f32) * 2.0'f32
    lookTY = (0.5'f32 - py / SH) * 2.0'f32
    lookX += (lookTX - lookX) * 0.3'f32
    lookY += (lookTY - lookY) * 0.3'f32
    if not dragOn: return
    let dx = px - dragX
    let dy = py - dragY
    if not dragMoved and dx * dx + dy * dy > 49.0'f32:
      dragMoved = true
      if mode == 1: stepBack(true)
    if dragMoved and mode == 0:
      tgt.rot   = max(-90.0'f32, min(90.0'f32, dragRot - dx * 0.18'f32))
      tgtPitch  = max(-PITCH_MAX, min(PITCH_MAX, dragPitch - dy * 0.12'f32))
    repaint()
  )

  scene.addHandler(evPointerUp, proc() =
    let (px, py) = pointerPos()
    let wasDrag = dragMoved
    dragOn = false
    dragMoved = false
    if not wasDrag: clickAt(px, py)
    padSlot = -1
    repaint()
  )

  page.addHandler(evKey, proc() =
    let c = lastKey().code
    if c == keyEscape:
      stepBack()
    elif c >= '1'.ord and c <= '8'.ord:
      approach(int32(c - '1'.ord))
    elif mode == 1:
      if c == keyLeft or c == 'a'.ord: stepWork(-1)
      elif c == keyRight or c == 'd'.ord: stepWork(1)
      elif c == keyUp or c == keyDown or c == keyBackspace: stepBack()
    elif c == keyEnter or c == keySpace:
      approach(nearest())
    elif c == keyHome:
      walkTo(Z_MAX - 40.0'f32)
    repaint()
  )

  scene.addHandler(evFrame, proc() =
    tick()
    repaint()
  )

  ready = true
  repaint()

proc yantra_main*() {.exportc: "yantra_main".} =
  main()
